# Legacy paper cuts: audit against v2

Every BUG-n, ENH-n and FOLLOWUP-n id in the legacy app (`/Users/geoff/repos/duo`: CHANGELOG.md, tasks.md, tasks-archive.md, docs/) checked against Duo v2 for the paper-cuts session (2026-10-09). Geoff dispositions each row on the paper-cuts page, https://claude.ai/artifact/CAq6xPAmo27e5sxCqBjFW9; his calls are recorded in the **Disposition** column as they arrive.

## Method

1. Built the id list from the legacy repo (512 ids) with each one's heading, changelog lines and the docs that mention it.
2. Triaged every id by area against v2's records (LR-n, LDI-n, F-n, C-n, Q-n, ENH-n, DL-n) and code (Sonnet), then a skeptic re-checked every likely-bug and feature call and spot-checked the rest (Opus).
3. A second skeptic per area challenged "handled" and "by construction" bug claims that rest on code reading alone; those and every likely bug were run live in isolated background Duo instances (scratch support folder and Claude config; the work-tree build).

Legacy ENH numbers are a separate series from v2's ENH-n in `docs/plan/enhancements.md`. "v1" is build plan §3a scope.

## Counts

- Reproduced in v2: 20
- Likely in v2: 11
- Feature, wanted?: 61
- Already handled: 131
- Not in v2, by construction: 109
- Not applicable: 180

## Reproduced in v2 (20)

| Id | Title | Area | Evidence | v2 record | Severity | v1 | Recommended | Disposition |

| BUG-043 | Find counts matches but does not scroll; arrow keys inert | editor | Code (papercuts worktree at 88c42b9, Vendor/codemirror/src/duo-editor.js): when properties are folded, propertiesDecorations (lines 725-728) swaps the whole frontmatter, from the opening fence to the closing fence or the blank line after it, for a single block HeadingWidget. The find panel (lines 1597-1603) and duo.find (line 2328) call CM6 findNext, which moves the selection into that hidden range and scrolls. Nothing sends setFolded(false) on a search or selection change. The only code that unfolds is the + button's addProperty (line 809) and the chevron (line 353). Because this is a custom replace decoration and not CM's foldState, CM's unfold-on-select doesn't apply. So any real Cmd-F on a note saved folded (the fold is remembered per document as state.json foldedProperties, findings.md line 933) gives the same result. The synthetic duo.find call takes the same findNext path as typing. Evidence images: f1.png shows "1 of 3" with "> PROPERTIES · 1" collapsed and no highlight. f3.png shows match 3 scrolled to and highlighted ("line zebra again"), so the original legacy failure (no scroll at all) is gone. f2.png (the table match) is blank and inconclusive. The code suggests tables are fine: blockDecorations (lines 1541-1545) draws a table raw when the caret is on one of its lines, so a match there shows. The find field's keydown handler binds only Enter and Escape; ArrowUp/Down are not handled. No DL or F record makes folded-properties-hides-match intended. I did not re-run: the code path settles it. **Live run:** reproduced in an isolated background run; skeptic confirmed: The legacy bug as titled (find never scrolls) is handled in v2: f3 proves it scrolls and highlights. What reproduces is a narrower case of the same symptom, a match inside folded properties. That's a real-use state, since the fold is remembered per document. The find field in the captures is empty because the query was set through the harness API, not typed. That's a test artefact and doesn't affect the result. The arrow-key half is unbound, but no record says whether v2 wants it, and Mac find fields usually don't step with the arrow keys. Treat it as an open question, not a confirmed bug. | S3-5 design (find in Duo's look) | low |  | Fix now: Fix: In Vendor/codemirror/src/duo-editor.js, have propertiesField or a transaction extender dispatch setFolded(false) and post propertiesFolded {folded:false} whenever the selection head moves inside frontmatterLines while it is folded, as addProperty does at line 809; this covers findNext and findPrevious. Optionally bind ArrowDown/ArrowUp in the find input's keydown (line 1598) to findNext and findPr |  |

With a tall tree, the sessions ScrollView shrinks to zero height and the tree overflows the pane, which clips it at both ends. The pane is hosted in RootView.swift:86 with no outer scroll. What fails is scroll-into-view plus the overflow, which the triage row had left unverified. **Live run:** reproduced in an isolated background run; skeptic confirmed: The legacy race (selecting before the listing loads) is fixed in v2. | BUG-072 | Blockquote double-Enter did not exit the quote | editor | The evidence folder repro/BUG-072/ is empty, but repro/e2.err exists. The claimed output is '> first quote\n>\n> \nafter' after two Enters. Code path: Vendor/codemirror/src/duo-editor.js:1923 uses markdown({base: markdownLanguage}) with the default keymap, so Enter runs insertNewlineContinueMarkup. Vendor/codemirror/node_modules/@codemirror/lang-markdown/dist/index.js:250-258 only lifts out of a Blockquote when the line is empty and the line above is also an empty quoted line ("Two aligned empty quoted lines in a row"). Otherwise it goes on to :259+, which keeps the quote going and trims the empty line to ">". So the claimed output is exactly what upstream code produces, and the outcome does not depend on the harness. A synthetic keydown on .cm-content reaches the same CM keymap handler as a real Return. No DL or F record decides how a quote should exit (grep for blockquote in decisions.md and findings.md finds nothing). Caveats: the repro stopped after two Enters. By the code, a third Enter (the second one on an empty quote line) removes both empty quote lines and exits. The triage row's "handled" verdict came from reading the code without running it, and it misread this branch. I did not re-run, because the code is deterministic. **Live run:** reproduced in an isolated background run; skeptic confirmed: This is upstream CodeMirror 6 behaviour: an empty quoted line makes a paragraph break inside the quote. It is not a Duo regression, and nothing is lost: one more Enter exits. The claim rates it medium; I rate it low because a further keystroke gets the user out. It differs from lists, where CM lifts out of a tight list on the first empty Enter, and that difference is exactly what the legacy complaint described. The nested quote (q2.md) and quote-then-list cases follow the same branch. |  | low |  | Fix now: Fix: In Vendor/codemirror/src/duo-editor.js, near the markdown() setup at :1923, add a Prec.high Enter binding ahead of insertNewlineContinueMarkup. When the cursor's line matches /^\s*(>\s?)+$/ and nothing follows the cursor, it should remove one quote level (or the whole "> ") and return true. Add an editor-test assertion that '> a', Enter, Enter leaves the second line with no '>'. |  |
| BUG-085 | Editor misses external writes; autosave squashes agent edits | editor | Code path (papercuts worktree at 88c42b9, Sources/DuoKit/Editor/DocumentEditor.swift): - open() at :112 calls saveNow(force: true) and then, in the same pass, sets url = B and calls load(B). - In saveNow at :311-316, the file on disk no longer matches diskBytes because the watcher's 150 ms settle hasn't run yet. saveNow therefore calls reconcile(A) and does not write. - reconcile's JS callback at :466 checks `self.url == file`. By then url is B, so the callback calls then() and returns. saveNow's continuation at :314 hits the same url check and stops. - Nothing is written, and kept[A] is never set because that only happens for a conflict or a removed file (:105-110). - duo.create(B) then replaces the buffer, so the edit is gone.  Harness check: the harness's user-type runs duo.userReplace (Vendor/codemirror/src/duo-editor.js:2282), which dispatches a change with userEvent "input.type". That posts the same "selection"/dirty message as real typing, so the synthetic events are not what causes the loss. The window being in the background doesn't matter either, because this logic is all timing between the watcher and the JS callbacks.  My re-runs were isolated: DUO_SUPPORT_DIR and CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR under /private/tmp/dpv-BUG-085, a copied workspace, run-live.sh with DUO_APP set to the papercuts build, and the instances quit by run-live's own SIGTERM by pid. - control (user-type, then doc:B.md 0.6 s later, no outside write): "alpha EDITED" was saved. - single (disk-write tick 0=>tick 1, then doc:B.md 0.6 s later): the edit survived, because the watcher had settled and merged first. - tight (user-type, disk-write, then +doc:B.md 20 ms after the write): editor-state showed buffer="# A\n\nalpha\n\ntick 1\n" with dirty=false. A.md on disk has no EDITED, and grep for "alpha EDITED" over the support dir found nothing. Logs: /private/tmp/dpv-BUG-085/{control,single,tight}.err  The original 50 ms continuous writer (repro/BUG-085/writer.py) also keeps diskChanged's settle re-arming, so diskBytes is always stale. That widens the window a lot, but it is not needed to trigger the loss.  Decisions: DL-77 and F-44 promise that saves are never blind and that switching documents never loses text. This loss contradicts them; no DL or F record makes it intended. **Live run:** reproduced in an isolated background run; skeptic confirmed: The real-use window is narrow: the user must have typed within the 1 s autosave delay, and an outside write must have landed within about 150 ms plus a JS round trip before the switch. It is most likely while a Claude session is actively editing the same note the user is typing in. The loss is silent and leaves no history copy. The original claim's "blind-write variant" was not run, and I didn't run it either. The legacy triage row said "handled"; it isn't for this path. A separate, harmless effect of the continuous writer: while writes come faster than the settle, the open buffer stays behind | LR-31, LR-32, LDI-3, LDI-4, LDI-5, LDI-9, F-44 | high |  | Fix now: Loses data or blocks work. Fix: In DocumentEditor.swift open() (around :105-112), stash the leaving file's buffer in kept[leaving] (text plus diskBytes as base) whenever the file on disk differs from diskBytes. load() already reconciles kept text when the user returns to the file. Alternatively, make the reconcile-then-save path at :311-316 and :466 finish against the captured file rather than bailing when url != file. |  |

| BUG-117 | SessionStart hook write must fail gracefully when settings.json is locked | updates-install | The code path is the same on main and in the papercuts build (no diff in Sources/DuoControl/Installer.swift). Line 169 reads the file with `(try? String(contentsOf: claudeMD, encoding: .utf8)) ?? ""`, so a missing file, an unreadable file and a non-UTF-8 file all come back as "". The write at line 186 is `Data(text.utf8).write(to: claudeMD, options: .atomic)`, which writes a temp file and renames it over the path. That replaces a symlink and needs only folder write permission, not file permission. I re-ran it myself with Helpers/duo2 install, using my own DUO_INSTALL_ROOT and DUO_SUPPORT_DIR under /private/tmp/dpv-BUG-117, with DUO_SOCKET/DUO_TOKEN pointed at nothing (no app instance; Geoff's Duo was not touched). (A) claude/CLAUDE.md as a symlink to ../dotfiles/CLAUDE.md: afterwards it is a regular -rw-r--r-- file and the dotfiles copy holds only "My rules A" with no block. (B) a file containing 'My rules caf\xe9': output was "CLAUDE.md block: added." and grep -c "My rules" gave 0. (C) a chmod 000 file: output was "added", and after chmod 644, grep -c "My rules" gave 0. These match the claim's out.txt. It is not a harness artefact: DUO_INSTALL_ROOT only moves claudeDir, and the app runs the same install() at every launch after consent. No DL or F record says this is intended; F-n for the editor says non-UTF-8 files are opened read-only and never saved (LR-30), and the installer breaks that rule. Note: the legacy row (a hook write to a locked settings.json aborting the install) is handled in v2. The claim's case E (chflags uchg) shows the failed step is reported and the rest of the install carries on. This is a sibling bug the repro found on the CLAUDE.md path. Case D (blockRemovedByUser set wrongly after a 000 file) follows from the same read and I did not re-run it. **Live run:** reproduced in an isolated background run; skeptic confirmed: Data loss happens only on the first install or whenever the block isn't already present, but install runs at every launch after consent. A non-UTF-8 or mode-000 CLAUDE.md is rare. A symlinked ~/.claude/CLAUDE.md (dotfiles managed with stow or chezmoi) is common, and the link is destroyed silently. Uninstall uses the same try? read, so it simply skips an unreadable file (safe), but its atomic write would also break a symlink. Scratch dirs I created: /private/tmp/dpv-BUG-117/{A,B,C}. No repo files were changed. | LR-55, DL-74, DL-78 | high |  | Fix now: Loses data or blocks work. Fix: In Sources/DuoControl/Installer.swift:169, read the file as Data. | BUG-145 | duo doc lacks focused per-subcommand --help | cli | The claim's evidence folder repro/BUG-145/ is empty, so I checked it myself. I ran the papercuts build's Helpers/duo2 with no running Duo, at HEAD 88c42b9. `duo2 doc --help` printed "duo2: unknown command 'doc --help'. Run `duo2 help`." with rc=64. Real use, not the test harness: this is plain argv parsing, with no synthetic events, window or login involved. Code (worktree): Sources/DuoControl/Actions.swift:413-421 resolve() tries a 2-word verb, then a 1-word verb. `aliases` (:424) maps `--help` and `-h` to `help` only when one of them is the first word. `doc` is not a verb, so resolve returns nil and the command fails (Sources/duo2/main.swift:16-17). For `<verb> --help`, Invocation.init (Actions.swift ~:440-460) stores `help` as an ordinary flag, and nothing rejects unknown flags. Sources/DuoKit/Control/AppModel+Control.swift:266-271 (.browserOpen) reads only inv[0], so with `--help` it calls newBrowserTab(nil) and returns "Opened a new browser tab." That matches the claimed live output. Separately, `duo2 help doc` prints the global help, because the family is named `docs` (ActionFamily, Actions.swift:10). No DL or F record makes trailing `--help` intended; DL-71 only says every UI action has a verb. **Live run:** reproduced in an isolated background run; skeptic confirmed: I didn't re-run the live `browser open --help` case. The code path confirms it without ambiguity, and re-running would only open a tab in a scratch instance. Medium, not high: nothing runs unless an agent adds `--help` to a verb that takes no required positional. With side-effecting verbs (`session new`, `browser open`, possibly `doc save`) this is a real hazard for agents, who habitually try `<cmd> --help`. Also found: `duo2 help doc` (singular) quietly falls back to the global help. | DL-71 | medium | in | Fix now: Fix: In Sources/DuoControl/Actions.swift resolve() (:413-421), check for `--help` or `-h` before anything else. Return `.help` with the family or verb words as its rest, so `<family> --help` and `<verb> --help` print usage. Make `help(family:)` (~:512) also accept a verb, matching a singular or plural family name (`doc` to `docs`) and printing a single verb's usage line. |  |

| BUG-035 | False 'Couldn't find Claude Code' banner when shell init is slow | terminal-chat | Read Sources/DuoKit/Terminal/Terminals.swift:35-54 myself. It checks 4 fixed folders (~/.local/bin, ~/.claude/local, /opt/homebrew/bin, /usr/local/bin) and does not scan Duo's PATH. It then runs `/bin/zsh -lc 'command -v claude'` and calls `p.waitUntilExit()` with no bound. The answer, including a nil, is cached at :22. It is called synchronously from app init (Sources/Duo/DuoApp.swift:76, which runs before the asynchronous warm), from the console's draw path (ConsoleStates.swift:53, AllProjectsPanes.swift:149) and from SettingsView.swift:21. Shell-level check in a scratch ZDOTDIR (/private/tmp/dpv-BUG-035/z), with claude on a PATH exported only in .zshrc: `zsh -lc 'command -v claude'` gave rc=1, so it was not found, while `zsh -lic` found it. With `sleep 3` in .zprofile, `zsh -lc` took 3.016 s, so the wait is synchronous and grows with .zprofile time. The claimant's own two live runs (r7/r8) did not isolate the lookup: HOME and ZDOTDIR did not change homeDirectoryForCurrentUser, so ~/.local/bin/claude was found. They showed neither the banner nor a hang, so the claim rests on the code alone. No DL or F record makes this intended. LR-19 asks for well-known folders, then the login shell, then a clear "not installed" state. The Settings recovery exists: Choose…, Use Found One, Look Again (S3-1). **Live run:** reproduced-by-code in an isolated background run; skeptic did not confirm: Not confirmed in the app. The demonstration is at the zsh level plus the code trace, and I did not launch Duo. The common case today, the native installer in ~/.local/bin, takes the fast path and is unaffected. The false not-found needs a claude reachable only through .zshrc. A blocking .zprofile freezes launch only for users whose claude is outside the four folders, and after that the cached result avoids repeats. The original title, "slow init gives a false banner", does not describe v2: v2's failure is waiting, or a false not-found for .zshrc-only installs, not a timeout. Scratch dir /priva | LR-19, S3-1, C-34 | medium | in | Fix later (ENH): Native installs in ~/.local/bin, the common case now, hit the fast path. The gaps are the missing dirs, no PATH scan and no -i, which give a false not-found with a manual fix, and the unbounded login-shell wait on the main thread, which can hang the app. Fix: add the dirs and a PATH scan, run the fallback with a timeout through the ClaudeVersion.output runner, try -lc then -lic, and don't cache a nil from a timeout. | Fix later: ENH-59 |
| BUG-094 | Pasting text with a trailing newline into a terminal auto-executes it | terminal-chat | The claim was traced through the code only. There was no live run, and the evidence folder repro/BUG-094/ is empty. I re-read the code path. /Users/geoff/repos/duo-v2/Sources/DuoKit/Terminal/Terminals.swift:251-306: GuardedTerminalView overrides only drag-and-drop (insertPaths at 296-299, bracketed when mode 2004 is on) and setFrameSize. It has no paste override, and neither does the papercuts worktree build. So Cmd+V goes to SwiftTerm MacTerminalView.swift:3415 paste, then pasteClipboard, then insertText(isPaste:true) at :2456, then pasteText. pasteText adds ESC[200~ brackets only when the program has turned on mode 2004, and nothing in this path trims a trailing newline. The code path matches the claim. Against "under real use": Geoff's $SHELL is /bin/zsh 5.9. His ~/.zshrc, ~/.zprofile and ~/.zshenv never mention bracketed paste, so zsh's line editor keeps its default 2004h, and a pasted trailing newline only lands in the edit buffer. Claude Code also turns on 2004. The bug needs a non-default setup (bash 3.2 as login shell, zle_bracketed_paste unset, or a raw program such as cat or ssh to an old host). Records: LR-15 holds the paste-strip clause, and legacy-requirements.md:5 and decisions.md DL-45 make LR-15 "no longer a rule". No DL or F record mandates or forbids stripping on paste. **Live run:** reproduced-by-code in an isolated background run; skeptic did not confirm: I did not re-run it. A live repro would need SHELL=/bin/bash and a real pasteboard write, which would overwrite Geoff's general pasteboard, and the code trace is unambiguous anyway. Rated likely rather than reproduced: the path exists, but it wasn't demonstrated, and it doesn't happen in the default or Geoff's configuration. It is arguably not a bug, because it is standard native terminal behaviour and DL-45 weakened the LR-15 mandate. The claim's severity of low and recommendation of fix-later are reasonable. | LR-15 (revised by DL-45), DL-68, DL-112 | low | in | Fix later (ENH): This is real for shells without bracketed paste, but those are rare on macOS, and every native terminal behaves the same way. | BUG-202 | Markdown tab mounts empty placeholder despite non-empty file | editor | The claim was never run. Legacy load race, handled in v2: - Sources/DuoKit/Editor/DocumentEditor.swift:93-97: didFinish sets pageReady and runs the pending load. - Lines 125-126 (open): if the page isn't ready, the load is stored as the pending closure, and the latest open wins. - Vendor/codemirror/dist/editor.html loads cm6.js with a plain synchronous `<script src>`, so `duo` exists before didFinish. - load() (lines 189-237) reads the bytes on the Swift side and calls duo.create(t). There is no empty-state placeholder to mount.  Crash path, a real gap in the code: - `grep -rn ProcessDidTerminate Sources` finds nothing. EditorController (line 10) is the WKNavigationDelegate but does not implement webViewWebContentProcessDidTerminate. - Only the initializer calls loadFileURL (line 72). The editor web view is one instance shared by every document. - `guard file != url` (line 102) means opening the same tab again never reloads. - pageReady is never reset, so after a crash every load() call and duo.text() call fails quietly. - saveNow returns on failure without writing (line 316), so nothing on disk is lost. Only edits typed in the second before the crash would be lost.  No WebContent kill was tried, by the claimant or by me, so a blank pane after a crash is inferred, not observed. WebContent crashes are rare in normal use. findings.md and decisions.md have no record covering this. **Live run:** reproduced-by-code in an isolated background run; skeptic did not confirm: The legacy symptom, an empty document from a load race on a non-empty file, does not exist in v2; for that, the triage row's "not-by-construction" stands. The claimant swapped in a different failure (a web process crash) that the legacy bug never described, then marked it reproduced without running anything. That crash gap is genuine in the code, so I kept it as "likely". A real test would SIGKILL the com.apple.WebKit.WebContent process belonging to an isolated instance's editor, which is hard to target safely without touching other WebKit clients, so I did not re-run. The same missing handler |  | medium |  | Fix later (ENH): Plausible gap, not shown live. Fix: In Sources/DuoKit/Editor/DocumentEditor.swift, implement webViewWebContentProcessDidTerminate on EditorController (the class starts at line 10). It should: - set pageReady to false - set pending to reload the current url - reload editor.html as at line 72, then re-send noteContext in didFinish as it |  |

| BUG-265 | Quitting one Duo unlinks another instance's live socket | cli | Code (papercuts worktree at 88c42b9, Sources/DuoKit/Control/ControlServer.swift): stop() (~:49-60) deletes the shared endpoint.json and socket only when endpoint.json still equals this instance's endpoint. That guard covers the legacy shutdown bug as stated. start() (~:25-30) calls unlink(path) on the shared socket every time, unless privateIfTaken is set ("the newest Duo answers"). DuoApp.swift:50 sets privateIfTaken only for capture runs (options.capturing). So a second non-capture instance unlinks A's socket and rewrites endpoint.json with B's token and pid. When B quits, stop() correctly removes B's own files, and A is left listening on an inode that no longer exists. The claimed run matches this exactly. endpointA.json shows A (pid 67762) on /private/tmp/dpc-cli-1/Duo/duo.sock. B was started with `open -g -n` on the same isolated support folder and without --capture; instB.err shows a background test launch with no "listening on private socket" line. Reported results: duo2 with A's token got "wrong token" while B ran; after B was sent SIGTERM, "couldn't connect (No such file or directory)" with A still alive. Even while B runs, A's terminals hold A's token in their environment (ChildEnvironment.control) and get "wrong token". Artefact check: the run used `open -n` to force a second instance on a scratch folder, which a user would not do. Info.plist has no LSMultipleInstancesProhibited, so a second copy of Duo.app opened from another path (a mounted DMG, a dev build) would start a second instance. That path was not demonstrated. C-18 records the takeover as intended ("one per user, newest answers") and fixes only scripted and capture runs. Nothing records that A is orphaned after B quits. No re-run was needed; the code path is clear. **Live run:** reproduced in an isolated background run; skeptic did not confirm: The legacy bug as worded ("a second instance's shutdown removed the first's socket") is handled in v2: stop() is guarded. The user-visible outcome, the first Duo left unreachable after the second quits, still happens, through the takeover at start that C-18 accepts as intended, so this is partly by design. It is reachable only when two non-capture Duos run under one user. The repro forced that with `open -n` on a scratch support folder. In real use it would need a second copy of the app opened, such as a DMG or dev build while the installed Duo runs. That is plausible during upgrades but not s | C-18 | medium |  | Fix later (ENH): Plausible gap, not shown live. Fix: In ControlServer.start() (Sources/DuoKit/Control/ControlServer.swift ~:25), when ControlEndpoint.discover finds another live Duo, either refuse the shared socket and fall back to the private socket for every launch, not just capture runs, or have the new instance tell the old one to step down. The repro's own log (repro/BUG-062/out.txt) shows something else: a SKILL.md edited by hand and set up with a matching skillHash, which doctor labels "from an older Duo", and which `duo2 install` then rewrites. The repro admits no older release was run. The rewrite is intended: any skill text that differs is refreshed at launch, and the guard at line 164 (`m.format <= format`) blocks only a downgrade of the format. The only thing left that relates to the legacy bug is a wording slip. Doctor says "from an older Duo" even when a newer build with the same format wrote the file. I'm rejecting the claim because the repro moved to a different issue (two builds overwriting each other) from the legacy one (a banner showing the wrong version). What it showed is the intended refresh-at-launch behaviour (DL-74), and the input was faked by hand. Flip-flopping between builds only happens when a dev build and a release run alternately, and nothing is lost: each launch rewrites the generated files. | BUG-148 | Main process crashes with EPIPE when stdout is closed | updates-install | The mechanism is real. In repro/BUG-148, run.sh starts papercuts' build/Duo.app/Contents/MacOS/Duo straight from the shell with `2>&1 \| head -1`. out.txt shows `pipestatus: 141 0`, meaning Duo was killed by SIGPIPE (128+13) on a later stderr write. plain.txt is just a run with a bad state name. Nothing in Sources handles SIGPIPE: the only signal call is `signal(SIGTERM, SIG_IGN)` at Sources/Duo/DuoApp.swift:139, and there is no SO_NOSIGPIPE. Real-use paths I checked, none of which get there: (1) A Finder, Dock or open(1) launch: stderr goes to /dev/null or a file. The repo's scripts use `open -g --stderr <file>`. (2) duo2 does not start Duo through a pipe. Its only Process call is atos, at Sources/duo2/main.swift:260. (3) The control server uses NWListener (Sources/DuoKit/Control/ControlServer.swift:33), and Network.framework handles a client that disconnects without raising SIGPIPE. (4) Duo opens no write pipes into child processes outside DuoChecks. ChatSession, Terminals, GitIgnoreOffer and SettingsView send child stderr to nullDevice, and terminals run on a PTY. docs/design/legacy-requirements.md:164 lists EPIPE guards as Electron artefacts. I didn't re-run it because the claimed output already shows the mechanism. **Live run:** reproduced in an isolated background run; skeptic did not confirm: The claim is accurate and already says it only happens when stderr is a pipe. Under the rule that confirmed means it happens in real use, it doesn't count: the failure needs a developer to launch the binary by hand into a pipe that closes early. | BUG-213 | Managed hook scripts vanish while settings.json still references them | updates-install | Re-read the code on the papercuts worktree at 88c42b9. No re-run was needed. (1) Hooks are per session only. Terminals.swift:190-197 hookArgs passes `--settings <events>/<id>.settings.json` to the claude processes Duo starts. HookEvents.swift:33-75 writes that file. Nothing goes into ~/.claude/settings.json, so other sessions can't carry a stale hook (LR-55). That rules out the legacy symptom. (2) Part 1 of the claim is real but self-heals. DuoApp.swift:131 calls InstallPrompt.run → Installer.install(cli:) (Installer.swift:202-216) at every launch, and that relinks the link to the running app's Helpers/duo2. Installer never checks isTransient; only doctor's update probe does (duo2/main.swift:233). So launching from /Volumes or AppTranslocation points the link there. Once that copy goes away the link dangles: out.txt shows rc=127. The next Duo launch relinks it. A disk image can't be ejected while Duo is running from it, so the link only dangles while Duo is closed, when duo2 couldn't reach Duo anyway. The visible difference is "no such file" instead of duo2's own "Duo isn't running". (3) Part 2 was not run. The edit and context hooks use the absolute app path (HookEvents.swift:52-60), so they break only if the app moves while a session it started is still running. That's unusual: Sparkle replaces the app at the same path, and quitting Duo ends its terminals. A command that exits 127 is a non-blocking hook error in Claude Code, so the edit itself still goes through. Harness artefact: the "build doctor" step in out.txt reached Geoff's real Duo ("Found Duo through environment: /Users/geoff/Library/Application Support/Duo/duo.sock", "Duo 34502, live, 21 projects") because DUO_SOCKET/DUO_TOKEN weren't set. It only read. The link lines came from the isolated DUO_INSTALL_ROOT. **Live run:** reproduced in an isolated background run; skeptic did not confirm: The claim moved from the legacy problem (global hook scripts deleted, errors in every session) to a different one (a symlink to a transient app copy). The legacy problem is handled by construction (LR-55, DL-74). The new problem is real but low: it only shows while Duo is closed and fixes itself at the next launch. I didn't reproduce Part 2 live; it's argued from the code only. The repro's doctor step wasn't isolated: it reached Geoff's live Duo socket, though it only read from it. | LR-55, DL-74 |  |  | — |  |

| ENH-133 | Shift+Return = soft newline in Claude tabs | terminal-chat | The claimed repro is a code trace only. It wasn't run live, and repro/ENH-133/ is empty. The trace itself is accurate: GuardedTerminalView (papercuts Sources/DuoKit/Terminal/Terminals.swift:251-306) has no keyDown or doCommand override, and none of the key monitors (PaneFocus.swift:43, DuoApp.swift:120, ChatKeys.swift:22) handles Shift+Return. Without kitty flags, SwiftTerm's doCommand(insertNewline) falls back to plain CR (MacTerminalView.swift:2381) and the Shift is dropped. The trace's premise is wrong, though: it assumes Claude doesn't push kitty flags because TERM_PROGRAM is removed (Terminals.swift:98). Claude Code 2.1.296 doesn't need TERM_PROGRAM. In its binary, the capability probe sends CSI ? u, and a reply marks extendedKeys true, which then emits `CSI <u` + `CSI >5u` + `CSI >4;2m` (functions i$t/ngt, tOs=">1u", eOs=">5u"). SwiftTerm at 6a955b0 answers CSI ? u (Terminal.swift:1419) and XTVERSION (Terminal.swift:5216). Live probe: I spawned claude 2.1.296 in a pty with TERM=xterm-256color, no TERM_PROGRAM and a scratch CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR (/private/tmp/dpv-ENH-133/probe.py), and answered CSI ? u the way SwiftTerm does. Claude then wrote ESC[>5u and ESC[>4;2m. With flags set, SwiftTerm's doCommand sends insertNewline as sendKittyFunctionalKey(.enter, mods from pendingKittyKeyEvent) (MacTerminalView.swift:2331-2333, pending set at 2155). The encoder emits CSI-u for Enter with modifiers when disambiguate is on (KittyKeyboardEncoder.swift:362-369), so Shift+Return goes out as CSI 13;2u, Claude's own Shift+Enter. Not run: a real keypress in Duo. The harness only has synthetic key actions, and a real one would need Geoff's OK for a foreground run. F-18 (findings.md:120) covers only the ESC CR path. **Live run:** reproduced-by-code in an isolated background run; skeptic did not confirm: The open question is Claude 2.1.219 on the work Mac. It isn't installed here (only 2.1.288-2.1.296), so I couldn't check whether that version probes for the kitty protocol. A plain shell tab still sends CR for Shift+Return, as most terminals do, and that's fine. LR-16's promised ESC CR remap is absent from the code, but in practice the kitty protocol does its job, so this is a records discrepancy and not a user-visible bug. The probe ran in /private/tmp/dpv-ENH-133 with a scratch config; I didn't touch Duo or ~/.claude. | LR-16, LDI-16, F-17, F-18 |  | in | — | Fix later: ENH-67 |

Code: TableWidget.ignoreEvent() returns false (papercuts Vendor/codemirror/src/duo-editor.js:1284), so CodeMirror handles mousedown and sets its own selection; the browser never makes a native one in the widget. blockDecorations (:1532-1548) switches the table to its Markdown when rawLines (:190, the anchor's line) lands in it. Re-run (my own scratch script /private/tmp/dpv-BUG-108/t.mjs): the built dist/editor.html in headless Chromium at 460 px, real Playwright mouse input on the same doc (intro line + 3x3 table). Pressing on cell 'b1' set anchor to 76 (the table's end) and drawn=false, so the source was showing. A drag across the cell left CodeMirror's selection and the DOM selection both at '\n\| b1 \| b2 \| b3 \|\n\| c1 \| c2 \| c3 \|'. A copy wrote text/plain with that same string. So copy matches what is highlighted, not 'intro line'. With the caret at 0, a double-click on 'b2' selected '\|---\|---\|---\|', a mis-landing (see notes). **Live run:** reproduced in an isolated background run; skeptic did not confirm: Intended per DL-113: with the caret in a table its Markdown shows, and option B (edit in the drawn table) was rejected. The legacy '[table]' cause (ProseMirror slice serialiser) doesn't exist in v2. A separate real issue turned up and is not this bug. A first click or drag on a drawn table puts the caret at the table's edge (posAtCoords on a block replace widget). The table then becomes source and shifts the layout, so the drag's head and a double-click land on the wrong text: double-click 'b2' selected the delimiter row, and the drag ran from table end to line 4 instead of the 'b1' cell. | BUG-212 | Entitlements miss device.gpu: no Metal in Duo-spawned terminals; [[ fallback to default vault | updates-install | v2 is unsandboxed and signs with Hardened Runtime and no entitlements file (scripts/release.sh:127-142; :142 only asserts no get-task-allow). Hardened Runtime is a property of each executable's own signature, not inherited across exec, and Metal needs no hardened-runtime entitlement (com.apple.security.device.gpu is not among Apple's hardened-runtime entitlements). Terminal children are separate binaries started directly (Terminals.swift:172-184: claude, or $SHELL -l), so their GPU access is governed by their own signatures, as in Terminal.app. Legacy's 'inherit' mechanism applied to Electron helper bundles/sandbox inheritance, which v2 does not have. The vault half is not-applicable (no vault in v1). |  |  |  | — |  |

| FOLLOWUP-049 | Navigator P1 perf tier (D3/D4/D5): incremental watch, row memo, stable git Map | files-tree | v2 has no chokidar watcher and no React tree. A 2s Timer (Sources/DuoKit/Model/AppModel.swift:390) drives refreshLive (AppModel.swift:397), which rebuilds the snapshot. treeFiles (LiveSnapshot.swift:374) reads only the top level and the folders the user opened (DL-105). F-134 / Q-79: toggleFolder reads that one folder directly, so expanding a folder costs one directory read and never tears down a watcher. No listeners pile up and no row memoization is needed. What remains is the cost of the full snapshot rebuild every 2s, which nobody has measured. | DL-105, F-134, Q-79 |  |  | — |  |
| FOLLOWUP-051 | Frontmatter edge stored as rel-path drops the graph edge; flip to [[wikilinks]] | vault-knowledge | DL-17 (decisions.md:27) deliberately writes quoted markdown links in frontmatter, never wikilinks; DL-150 uses the same shape for references/depends_on/sessions. v2 has no Duo-side link graph that depends on [[ ]] extraction, so no Duo edge is lost. | FOLLOWUP-054 | PR#98 interaction-review carry-over findings (Home/nav/worktree/terminal) | dev-process | Home attribution by re-encoded live cwd is gone: v2 mints --session-id and reads agents --json, and the cwd-inference layer is dropped (legacy-requirements.md:153). LR-8 re-checks liveness at click time. The UUID regex item can't arise: v2 passes the id as an argv element, not through a shell (Terminals.swift:176, 180: startProcess(args: ["--resume", id, ...])). Navigator staleness maps to LR-36 watcher hygiene. Terminal min-cols: Terminals.swift:301-304 clamps the view frame up to minimumSize, so the PTY size follows the clamped frame and no resize is dropped, which is the fix legacy wanted. Worktree probe cost and the pill test gap are code that only exists in legacy. The record-number collision does recur in v2, but it is handled: CLAUDE.md 'Record numbers are shared across sessions' gives the renumber procedure, and scripts/check-records.sh exists. | LR-8, LR-36 |  |  | — |  |

## Legacy detail

What each id was in legacy and how it was fixed, for the rows above.

- **BUG-001** (bug): Ctrl+Tab with terminal focus cycled the browser tabs instead of terminal tabs. *Fix:* Pane-aware key dispatch in the renderer shortcut handler.
- **BUG-002** (bug): With the Electron WebContentsView holding OS focus, ⌘T went nowhere and ⌘F/⌘G never reached the renderer's find bar. *Fix:* Added keys to wireKeyForwarding allowlist; address-bar focus reclaim for ⌘T/⌘F.
- **BUG-003** (feature): Active-pane indicator was too faint to tell which pane had the keyboard. *Fix:* Shipped: indicator made more visible (no detailed section).
- **BUG-004** (bug): The pane-focus toggle chord left keyboard input routed wrongly afterwards (Electron renderer/xterm focus race). *Fix:* Fixed in legacy by reworking focus handling (see BUG-048 root cause: xterm focus listener racing the toggle).
- **BUG-005** (bug): Synthetic Cmd+End key events fell through to the Electron application menu and opened About. *Fix:* CLI translated Cmd+End/Home/PageDown/PageUp to Mac-native combos.
- **BUG-006** (bug): Renderer-DOM pill was occluded by the native WebContentsView at the compositor level. *Fix:* Moved pill into the page DOM via CDP injection.
- **BUG-007** (bug): Files deleted on disk stayed in the navigator until reload; chokidar unlink events fired but no renderer subscriber, then a resub gap dropped events. *Fix:* Renderer installed a files watch on cwd plus expanded folders and refreshed parents; hardened with a refresh after the watcher attaches.
- **BUG-008** (bug): Keyboard routing for new-tab shortcuts varied by pane; spec flip-flopped between pane-aware and universal. *Fix:* Made ⌘T new browser tab and ⌘⇧T new Claude tab everywhere (later reversed again).
- **BUG-009** (bug): The + button wrote 'claude' into a shell before its prompt was ready. BUG-010 then left a cosmetic echo above the prompt. *Fix:* waitForPtyReady waited for the first PTY data, later a prompt-tail regex.
- **BUG-010** (bug): waitForPtyReady resolved on the shell's first PTY byte, so the typed `claude` echoed on a bare line above the prompt (cosmetic). *Fix:* Strip ANSI and match a prompt-tail regex on the last 160 chars before typing the command.
- **BUG-011** (followup): In the smoke checklist, BUG-011 only appears as the example of a computer-use screenshot that takes 5-15 s, so a state that dismisses itself after 3 s is missed. It is a testing note, not a product bug. *Fix:* A note in the smoke checklist: check transient UI through the DOM or the CLI, not screenshots.
- **BUG-012** (bug): Keys pressed while an embedded canvas iframe or other renderer surface had focus never reached the app-level handler. This is the BUG-012/013/014 family. *Fix:* Prevention in the architecture: three documented keyboard escape patterns, one of which every new tab type must use, recorded in the CLAUDE.md plumbing checklist.
- **BUG-013** (bug): TipTap focus swallowed the ⌘T keystroke, so it never opened a new browser tab. *Fix:* Shortcuts escape the editor; closed by the 'preventative architecture' (global shortcuts fire from editor focus).
- **BUG-014** (bug): ⌃Tab and other global shortcuts did not fire from TipTap focus. *Fix:* Same preventative architecture as BUG-013.
- **BUG-015** (bug): Empty comment rail column shown when an HTML canvas had no comment threads. *Fix:* Gated rail on railThreads.length > 0.
- **BUG-016** (bug): Pasted HTML carried inline color/background styles into the editable canvas, giving brown-on-brown text. *Fix:* Paste handler scrubs inline color/background/class styles.
- **BUG-017** (bug): nativeTheme.themeSource was hardcoded to light, so system mode never followed the OS. *Fix:* Renderer pushes its mode over IPC and main sets nativeTheme.themeSource.
- **BUG-018** (bug): New browser tab loaded the default FAQ landing, duplicating the first tab. *Fix:* IPC addTab defaults to about:blank; constructor keeps FAQ for first tab.
- **BUG-019** (bug): After new tab, focus did not land in the address bar (React mount timing). *Fix:* Two nested requestAnimationFrames before focus().
- **BUG-020** (bug): Last/first browser tab had no close X and Cmd+W did nothing, without being pinned. *Fix:* closeTab opens a fresh about:blank first, then closes the requested tab.
- **BUG-021** (bug): Keyboard-shortcut hook captured a stale tabs list, so tabs hydrated by session restore were skipped by Ctrl-Tab. *Fix:* Shipped v0.4.3: hook reads tabs and activeTabId through refs (stale-closure fix).
- **BUG-022** (bug): Fresh editable canvas mounted unfocused; needed an extra click to type. *Fix:* RenderedCanvas calls body.focus() on mount.
- **BUG-023** (bug): Body was a 720px column so clicks in flanking margins didn't place a cursor. *Fix:* Body fills viewport; content in a 720px main.
- **BUG-024** (bug): Two selection pills rendered at the same anchor and overlapped. *Fix:* Comment button stacks below, Send above.
- **BUG-025** (bug): Folder row was one button, so chevron click both toggled and selected. *Fix:* Split chevron into its own hit target with stopPropagation (Stage 26 PR 1).
- **BUG-026** (bug): tiptap-markdown parsed all pastes inline, so block markdown landed as one code block. *Fix:* Added MarkdownPaste extension doing block-aware parse.
- **BUG-027** (feature): Chrome-parity reopen-closed-tab; legacy added a closedTabs stack (cap 10) per BrowserManager. *Fix:* Shipped: closedTabs stack and reopenLastClosed; pane-aware ⌘⇧T (legacy CL:261 also shows it silently ate ⌘Z for six releases).
- **BUG-028** (bug): Esc in the navigator inline rename input did nothing; only Enter or click-away left rename mode. *Fix:* Escape branch calls stopPropagation, sets cancelledRef, blurs the input, blur handler skips commit.
- **BUG-029** (bug): Custom HTML context menu on Pinned rows at the window bottom overflowed and clipped. *Fix:* ContextMenu measured itself in useLayoutEffect and flipped up/left.
- **BUG-030** (bug): CLI nav pin/unpin edited nav-pins.json but the renderer read it once on mount, so the Pinned section went stale. *Fix:* Added NAV_PINS_CHANGED push channel broadcast from IPC and socket paths.
- **BUG-031** (bug): Dragging the split divider over an iframe or WebContentsView stalled because the web surface trapped mousemove. *Fix:* Transparent full-screen overlay mounted during drag (iframes); browser-pane (WCV) coverage left unfixed.
- **BUG-032** (bug): HTML canvas iframe remount/agent edits pulled focus from the terminal mid-typing. *Fix:* shouldStealFocus prop gated on working column focus.
- **BUG-033** (bug): A queued autosave could fire while an agent-write banner was pending and write a stale snapshot over the agent's edit. *Fix:* v1: autosave blocked while pendingWrite is set; v2 deferred to reconciliation work (Stage 16).
- **BUG-034** (bug): Smart-blank onboarding card showed over content of existing HTML files. *Fix:* Removed the placeholder install.
- **BUG-035** (bug): The resolver spawned a login shell with a 5s timeout. A slow rc file produced a false 'not installed' banner. *Fix:* Check known install dirs (~/.local/bin, ~/.npm-global/bin, /opt/homebrew/bin, /usr/local/bin, ~/.volta/bin, ~/.bun/bin, ~/bin) and every PATH entry with X_OK first. Then fall back to the shell with a 15s timeout, fastest flag set (-l) before -l -i (tasks-archive.md:3940-3946).
- **BUG-036** (bug): Cmd+T always opened a browser tab. The owner wanted a pane-aware chord: a shell tab from terminal focus, a browser tab elsewhere. *Fix:* Pane-aware dispatch: Cmd+T from a terminal opens a shell in the front tab's launch cwd, and Cmd+Shift+T opens a Claude tab.
- **BUG-037** (bug): Click in iframe placed a cursor but focusedColumn stayed on terminal, so ⌃Tab/⌘T acted on the wrong pane. *Fix:* Capture-phase mousedown forwarder to set focusedColumn.
- **BUG-038** (bug): Five instances: stale closures, then the working-pane cycle used BrowserManager's list only so file tabs were skipped. *Fix:* Final fix dispatched an event so WorkingPane cycles its merged file+browser list in strip order.
- **BUG-039** (bug): Relaunch tried to reopen a file tab whose file was deleted and showed an ENOENT error in the tab. *Fix:* Shipped v0.5.3: files.exists IPC; hydration drops missing file tabs silently and logs the dropped paths.
- **BUG-040** (bug): external-domains.json was only an agent convention; address-bar/link/redirect navigations bypassed it. *Fix:* will-navigate/will-redirect/popup interceptors route matches to the system browser.
- **BUG-041** (bug): Right-clicking empty space under the tree gave no menu. *Fix:* Wrapper onContextMenu gated on target==currentTarget with a trimmed root menu (New file/folder, terminal, Reveal).
- **BUG-042** (bug): WebContentsView clicks never reached renderer JS so pane focus state stayed on terminal. *Fix:* webContents focus event pushes BROWSER_FOCUS_GAINED.
- **BUG-043** (bug): Cmd-F found matches but did not scroll to the current one; arrow keys in the find input did nothing. *Fix:* Native scrollIntoView on the decoration; ArrowDown/Up in the find input.
- **BUG-044** (bug): Find input text was unreadable in dark mode due to an inert Tailwind color family and focus:bg-white. *Fix:* Added paper color family; removed focus bg-white.
- **BUG-045** (bug): Tab context menu for file:// tabs was hidden behind the WebContentsView; owner also wanted local HTML to open in canvas not browser. *Fix:* setOverlayMuted collapses the WCV to 1x1 while the menu is open.
- **BUG-046** (bug): TipTap editor was re-mounted per tab switch, costing 1-2s. *Fix:* Shipped v0.5.4: all file-tab renderers stay mounted, display-toggled.
- **BUG-047** (bug): Class bug: native WCV paints above any renderer DOM overlay (menus, pill, find bar). *Fix:* Mute-and-restore, move overlays above bounds, or inject into page DOM.
- **BUG-048** (bug): Accelerator's pre-IPC focus reclaim fired xterm's focus listener, flipping focusedColumn before the toggle read it. *Fix:* v3: main no longer reclaims focus on the chord; renderer handles it (3 rounds).
- **BUG-049** (bug): Trash flow reused the pinned-tab-close confirm component, giving incoherent text. *Fix:* Parameterized the confirm with title/body/label.
- **BUG-050** (bug): HTML context menu rendered inside the tab strip stacking context and dropped behind the editor. *Fix:* Portaled to document.body, z-index 1000; later replaced by native menus (ENH-050).
- **BUG-051** (bug): Toggling read-only off/on left body contenteditable=true because wire() only added attributes. *Fix:* Explicit else branch clears contenteditable and related attributes.
- **BUG-052** (bug): Stage 27 editor:open data-mode=canvas showed no toolbar or read-only strip on locked canvases. *Fix:* Shipped c010ef9: a Read-only/locked indicator was added.
- **BUG-053** (bug): nav:reveal navigated async then selected before the listing loaded (and on the wrong pane for ~/.claude paths). *Fix:* Atomic revealAndSelect action; route ~/.claude paths to the user-claude pane.
- **BUG-054** (bug): The canvas verb set the React focus indicator but never called term.focus(), so keystrokes did not reach the terminal. *Fix:* Dispatched a duo-terminal-focus CustomEvent that calls term.focus().
- **BUG-055** (bug): Mousedown forwarder for pane focus was gated inside !readOnly, so read-only canvases did not take focus on click. *Fix:* Shipped 11b3417: moved the listener out of the readOnly gate.
- **BUG-056** (bug): In-page Send pill appeared on selection even with no live Claude session, so the action was dead; recurring regression. *Fix:* Shipped 70fd53a plus a regression test on the __duoClaudeLive guard.
- **BUG-057** (bug): Pinned tabs vanished after quit or upgrade; session state did not persist pins. *Fix:* pins.json made authoritative; pins reopened on boot (main for browser, renderer for files).
- **BUG-058** (bug): Native WebContentsView paints above renderer DOM so the HTML menu was clipped by browser content. *Fix:* Mute-WCV hack, then retired by ENH-050 native menus.
- **BUG-059** (bug): Opening the same local file twice created two working-pane tabs. *Fix:* De-dupe by path in openFileSmart and the CLI openTab for file:// URLs.
- **BUG-060** (bug): Typing ``` fences did not become a code block until a trailing space. *Fix:* FencedCodeBlockEnter extension.
- **BUG-061** (bug): Typing '- ' in the contentEditable canvas did not make a list, because Chromium turns the trailing space into nbsp and the regex missed it. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.4: used \s in the trigger regexes, hand-rolled list conversion, and Tab/Shift-Tab indent.
- **BUG-062** (bug): Banner said 'currently from v0.6.0' while app was 0.5.7; installed-files receipt version differed from app version and the wording read as Duo's own version. *Fix:* Copy rewritten to show both versions ('files are from Duo vX, you're running vY'). Receipt/app mismatch only in dev.
- **BUG-063** (bug): The legacy smoke-walk generator pulled backtick literals out of step text, leaving a gap in the prose. *Fix:* isTrailingCmd() in the smoke-walk generate.mjs: only commands that end a sentence become Copy blocks.
- **BUG-064** (bug): In-renderer confirm modal was clipped and dimmed patchily over the browser view. *Fix:* ENH-050 moved confirms to dialog.showMessageBox system sheets.
- **BUG-065** (bug): Hooks placed after an early return in the React Breadcrumb crashed the whole renderer when toggling edit mode. *Fix:* Lifted hooks above the return; added a React ErrorBoundary.
- **BUG-066** (bug): SVG viewBox cut the top of the glyph and fill was hardcoded orange. *Fix:* Corrected viewBox, fill=currentColor.
- **BUG-067** (bug): `duo open` always routed file paths to the browser pane. *Fix:* Smart routing: .md to editor, HTML honours meta, URLs to browser.
- **BUG-068** (bug): Working strip's + cluster sat inside the overflow scroller and was hidden when many tabs / panned. *Fix:* Cluster moved outside the scroller as a sticky sibling, mirroring the terminal strip.
- **BUG-070** (bug): The srcdoc iframe's about:blank phase caused wire() to lock onto a disposable body, so the real doc was never made editable. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.4 (v3): wire() bails on the about:blank document.
- **BUG-071** (bug): After a browser-pane link opened a canvas, OS focus stayed in the WebContentsView so Ctrl-Tab did nothing until a re-click. *Fix:* mainWindow.webContents.focus() after sendEdit.
- **BUG-072** (bug): Enter on an empty line in a blockquote did not lift out, unlike lists (took 3 iterations). *Fix:* Block-ops fix with <br> filler in contentEditable.
- **BUG-073** (feature): Wanted '-' to render a dashed bullet instead of a round one in the canvas. *Fix:* Unfixed (filed).
- **BUG-074** (bug): Selected row used white text on a translucent fill that vanished on the light paper surface; opacity modifier produced invalid CSS. *Fix:* Solid accent fill with white text, no radius.
- **BUG-075** (bug): Chord matched e.key with shift (impossible) and Cmd-backslash is eaten by 1Password autofill; moved to Cmd-slash. *Fix:* e.code-based matching, chord changed to Cmd-/ and Cmd-Shift-/.
- **BUG-076** (bug): switchTab never focused the new WebContentsView, leaving OS focus on a 1x1 hidden view and cycle keystrokes drifting. *Fix:* BrowserManager.switchTab calls webContents.focus().
- **BUG-077** (bug): The owner saw tab cycling lag on rapid presses. The cause was the IPC round trip from renderer to main. An optimistic ref update only helped when presses were silently dropped. *Fix:* Unfixed; deferred 'if it persists'.
- **BUG-078** (bug): Constructor boot-default tab plus default-pin restore both re-opened the FAQ each launch. *Fix:* Both gated on !hasPersistedSession.
- **BUG-079** (bug): Recurring Electron-era latency in tab cycling; synthetic tests found no code-path delay, left instrumented, never root-caused. *Fix:* Unfixed (instrumented, deferred).
- **BUG-080** (bug): Tailwind prose styled <strong> near-black on dark paper. *Fix:* Explicit rule so strong inherits the ink color.
- **BUG-081** (bug): The hover Comment pill was gated on a Claude session; the hover-pill UX was replaced with a toolbar button, a shortcut and a context-menu entry. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.7.
- **BUG-082** (bug): Sidecar comments loaded asynchronously and a stale useMemo dependency left the rail hidden after reopen. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.7: bump threadsTick after the sidecar read and on ready.
- **BUG-083** (bug): Rail comments had no highlight on the anchored canvas text and no bidirectional click-to-focus. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.7 (canvas) and polished in v0.7.2 (markdown active-thread tint).
- **BUG-084** (bug): Electron default View menu reload roles bound Cmd-R to webContents.reload() on the main window. *Fix:* Removed reload/forceReload roles; later Cmd-R reloads only the active browser tab.
- **BUG-085** (bug): Open editor did not pick up external writes and autosave could overwrite agent edits; later a recurring false-positive conflict arc through BUG-166. *Fix:* Echo checks, then one shared reconcile path for editor and canvas.
- **BUG-086** (bug): The walk page opened as a canvas, so its Copy buttons were blocked and the owner pasted text by hand. *Fix:* Procedural: the skill re-checks how the page mounted after opening it.
- **BUG-087** (bug): Comment-rail threads past #1 did not highlight; new bullets cloned the parent data-duo-id so comments anchored to the same element. *Fix:* installAutoStampIds de-duplicates cloned ids.
- **BUG-088** (bug): contentEditable split an li and cloned its data-duo-id; the auto-stamp observer skipped the duplicate, so the new bullet had no unique anchor. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.7: duplicate-id detection in idInjector.
- **BUG-089** (bug): paintAnchors stripped and re-stamped the attribute on each keystroke, causing a one-frame flicker. *Fix:* Filed with a fix path (only update changed attributes); later superseded by the BUG-083 and BUG-088 work.
- **BUG-090** (bug): Cloned li elements shared one data-duo-id, so the thread builder bucketed separate comments into one thread. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.7: same duplicate-id fix as BUG-088.
- **BUG-091** (bug): Right-click on a working-pane tab did not reliably show Move to Split View; later also excluded browser tabs. *Fix:* Resolved by Phase 3c plumbing; entry extended to browser tabs.
- **BUG-092** (bug): Scripted HTML/browser pages moved to aux slot landed in a canvas that blocked scripts. *Fix:* Aux slot given a real Chromium tab (BrowserManager auxTabId).
- **BUG-093** (bug): Right-click Move to Split View sometimes crashed the Electron renderer; never reproduced, instrumentation only. *Fix:* Unfixed (owner-blocked, FOLLOWUP-013).
- **BUG-094** (bug): Pasting a copied command that ended in a newline into zsh without bracketed paste ran it at once, with no chance to edit. *Fix:* A capture-phase paste listener strips trailing CR/LF. Internal newlines stay.
- **BUG-095** (bug): Click in aux-pinned browser tab switched the main pane's active tab. *Fix:* BROWSER_FOCUS_GAINED carries slot; only main slot flips activeWorking.
- **BUG-096** (bug): Closing the last main-strip browser tab while another was pinned to aux left the aux blank. *Fix:* closeTab next-active picker skips aux tab; spawns about:blank if needed.
- **BUG-097** (bug): TipTap placeholder squeezed into a narrow column on first open of an empty .md. *Fix:* white-space nowrap / word-break CSS.
- **BUG-098** (bug): Tab context-menu Move to Trash on a file already gone from disk surfaced the macOS 'doesn't exist' error and left the tab open. *Fix:* Pattern-match ENOENT/doesn't exist and proceed to close the tab silently.
- **BUG-099** (bug): Rapid consecutive saves raced the single lastSavedBody echo baseline, raising a false conflict. *Fix:* Set of recently written bodies with a 2s TTL.
- **BUG-100** (bug): The CDP bridge attached to one WebContents, so the Send pill never appeared in the aux browser pane. *Fix:* Unfixed (deferred, CDP multi-attach refactor); BUG-133 and BUG-134 later kept bindings on every tab.
- **BUG-101** (bug): Verbs returned {ok:true} but the new tab wasn't focused or shown (React setState timing). *Fix:* Read latest committed state and push a focus-gained event to activate the tab.
- **BUG-102** (bug): Overlay-mute shrank the aux WebContentsView to 1x1 under the palette, blanking it. *Fix:* Unfixed (low, owner non-urgent).
- **BUG-103** (bug): Typography plugin drew curly quotes on blockquotes. *Fix:* CSS fix shipped v0.6.12.
- **BUG-104** (bug): A chord edit round-tripped through tiptap-markdown, normalised whitespace and tripped the conflict check. *Fix:* Normalise trailing whitespace in the compare.
- **BUG-105** (bug): navigator.clipboard.writeText silently rejected inside native NSMenu callbacks; Copy path did nothing. *Fix:* Routed through main-process clipboard IPC; added Copy URL/path to aux slot menu.
- **BUG-106** (bug): Opening a missing path made a tab and an editor that errored on read. *Fix:* Pre-create the file so an empty editor mounts.
- **BUG-107** (bug): Serialiser turned '# Index\n\n' into '# Index\n' so the first save looked like an outside change. *Fix:* normalize() in the compare (trailing whitespace, later BOM).
- **BUG-108** (bug): ProseMirror serialised a cell-only clipboard slice as '[table]'. *Fix:* TableCellCopy extension returning textBetween.
- **BUG-109** (bug): After a new browser tab the web view kept OS focus, so the address bar had no caret. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 9: reclaim focus before the rAF focus chain.
- **BUG-110** (bug): Two walks shared the key worksheet:smoke-walk-v0.6.10, so stale state overwrote the notes typed in one of them. *Fix:* Key by manifest basename instead of version.
- **BUG-111** (bug): A cloud agent built ENH-111 (image viewer) instead of the asked ENH-108 (paste image); fixed by shipping both. *Fix:* Shipped ENH-108 alongside.
- **BUG-112** (bug): Every mounted editor answered the IPC and the first reply won, so an arbitrary tab came back. *Fix:* isActive gate so only the active tab replies.
- **BUG-114** (bug): The Node CLI threw on EPIPE when stdout closed early under a pipe. *Fix:* process.stdout.on('error') maps EPIPE to exit 0 in cli/duo.ts.
- **BUG-115** (bug): Re-surface of BUG-107 turned out to be a test fixture writing the file after open. *Fix:* Closed as not a regression.
- **BUG-116** (bug): Release script picked alphabetically-last Duo-*.dmg (0.6.8 > 0.6.12) and launch-validated the old DMG. *Fix:* Pass the explicit versioned DMG path.
- **BUG-117** (bug): Install wrote a hook into ~/.claude/settings.json; enterprise-locked/read-only file could abort the whole install. *Fix:* try/catch around the hook install; rest of install continues.
- **BUG-118** (bug): The v0.6.12 cut committed an out-of-date cli/duo binary that did not match a fresh rebuild. *Fix:* A git diff --quiet cli/duo guard after build:cli in the cut skill.
- **BUG-119** (bug): Chokidar's fsevents native addon torn down after Node env, crashing with SIGABRT on each quit. *Fix:* Dispose pty manager and files service in before-quit so the callback is released while the mutex is alive.
- **BUG-121** (bug): Guards left over from the FAQ tab spawned a new about:blank whenever the last or only non-aux tab closed, in a loop. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 16: zero tabs became a supported state.
- **BUG-122** (bug): Save pre-reconcile compared against a baseline where tiptap-markdown HTML-escaped <, >, &; the baseline diverged from disk on files with HTML comments, so false banners. *Fix:* Six hypotheses; fix 6 normalised the escapes in the echo compare.
- **BUG-123** (bug): ProseMirror CellSelection was created but never painted (tables.css not imported), so multi-cell drag looked impossible. *Fix:* Imported/added .selectedCell overlay CSS; cross-boundary drag deferred.
- **BUG-124** (bug): Diagnostic log wrote to ~/.claude/duo/logs/ which was never mkdir-p'd, flooding stderr with ENOENT. *Fix:* Boot-time mkdir -p of the logs dir.
- **BUG-125** (bug): chokidar reported symlink-resolved paths, renderer's path-equality guard dropped every event, so editors stayed stale for /tmp paths; later, injected data-duo attrs caused false conflicts. *Fix:* Remap watcher paths to the caller's path; normalize Duo-injected attrs before comparing baseline.
- **BUG-126** (bug): Chromium's native find broke on a contentEditable srcdoc iframe; fixed with a custom CSS Highlight find bar. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 17: added pageFind.ts and PageFindBar.
- **BUG-127** (bug): Pasting markdown text into TipTap produced code blocks or squashed tables due to slice fitting and HTML clipboard path. *Fix:* Closed slice + transformPastedHTML to detect thin-wrapped markdown.
- **BUG-128** (bug): A research HTML page appeared blank during a walk. It did not reproduce and was later traced to BUG-129, where the path resolved to a bogus URL. *Fix:* Closed as no-repro by construction after BUG-129.
- **BUG-129** (bug): Missing target opened a blank tab silently. *Fix:* Return ok:false 'File not found' and open no tab.
- **BUG-130** (bug): Browser-pane tabs on file:// URLs went stale when the agent edited the file; the user had to press Cmd-R. *Fix:* Filed as an architectural item (L2-PLAYGROUND-AUTORELOAD); a chokidar watcher on the active file:// tab was proposed.
- **BUG-131** (bug): A key-forwarding list matched plain Cmd-A (intended for Cmd-Shift-A) and sent it to the renderer, so textareas in the browser pane never got select-all. *Fix:* Shipped v0.7.0: fixed the unconditional KeyA match.
- **BUG-132** (bug): Navigator right-click Open on GitHub did nothing; rev2 fixed the peer-repo case where an outer repo shadowed the child repo. *Fix:* Root cause: files.openExternal was shell.openPath (local only). Added URL-aware FILES_OPEN_EXTERNAL_URL IPC; handler prefers the child repo over the outer one.
- **BUG-133** (bug): The live-Claude flag was pushed only to the one CDP-attached tab, so other tabs showed the pill with no Claude open. *Fix:* Shipped v0.7.0: BrowserManager.broadcastClaudeLive to all tabs.
- **BUG-134** (bug): The pill's CDP binding was removed when the bridge detached from the previous tab, so clicks on other tabs did nothing. *Fix:* Shipped v0.7.0: CdpBridge no longer detaches, and addTab attaches CDP on each tab.
- **BUG-135** (bug): A folder inside a larger repo (~/Documents/GitHub/stoop under a ~/Documents repo) showed the ribbon and GitHub menu items claiming the outer repo. *Fix:* Suppress the ribbon when climbing from cwd to the repo root crosses a folder holding 2+ peer-repo children.
- **BUG-136** (bug): Electron's PATH lacked /opt/homebrew/bin so gh wasn't found and the clone modal showed a false not-signed-in banner. *Fix:* Prepend WELL_KNOWN_BIN_DIRS to PATH for all git/gh calls.
- **BUG-137** (bug): [text](url) input rule kept the URL instead of the text, links had no tooltip, and Cmd-K was shadowed. *Fix:* Custom InputRule, title attribute, extension priority bump, extendMarkRange.
- **BUG-138** (feature): Four-phase chapter adding inline CriticMarkup comments, suggestions, a rail and accept/reject in the TipTap editor. *Fix:* Shipped over several phases in v0.7.x.
- **BUG-139** (bug): The TipTap editor stripped frontmatter on load and re-joined it on save, so users could not see or edit it. A Properties panel was added later, collapsed by default, with click-to-expand and a raw-YAML toggle. *Fix:* A collapsible Properties panel with Edit raw. Q4 set it collapsed by default, Q5 added click-to-expand, and the textarea grows with its content.
- **BUG-141** (bug): Banner about existing SessionStart hooks read as though Duo duplicated entries every update; install behaviour was correct. *Fix:* Banner reworded ('Duo only replaces its own entry').
- **BUG-142** (bug): The --reply-to path nested a malformed token, so the editor did not update after doc comment. *Fix:* Closed by BUG-143 (proper addCommentReply).
- **BUG-143** (bug): CLI usability: replying to a comment required an anchor. *Fix:* --reply-to alone now works.
- **BUG-144** (bug): A path-mismatch branch in each mounted editor replied inconsistently with layout. *Fix:* Same isActive gating.
- **BUG-145** (bug): Only a ~200-line global help existed. *Fix:* Added `duo doc --help` with a short subcommand list.
- **BUG-146** (bug): Skill docs didn't tell agents which comment verb to use for canvas versus editor. *Fix:* Added a 'Comment disambiguation' section to SKILL.md.
- **BUG-147** (bug): Skill had no reference for the comment lifecycle. *Fix:* Added skill/references/comments.md.
- **BUG-148** (bug): console.log to a closed stdout threw EPIPE in the Electron main process and showed a crash dialog. *Fix:* Swallow EPIPE on stdout/stderr in main.
- **BUG-149** (bug): `duo navigate` took URLs only and gave ERR_INVALID_URL for a path. *Fix:* Helpful error pointing to `duo reveal`.
- **BUG-150** (bug): Reinstall only removed marked hook entries; unmarked duplicates survived, causing a permanent 'other hooks' banner and priming injected twice. *Fix:* Dedupe unmarked entries with the same Duo command.
- **BUG-151** (bug): Switching workspace asked a misleading double-confirm that wrote to the wrong file. *Fix:* Prompt removed; autosave mirror plus pre-switch flush handles persistence.
- **BUG-152** (bug): Loading a .duo-workspace restored only pinned tabs, dropping other browser tabs. *Fix:* Switch now restores all browser tabs from the workspace state.
- **BUG-153** (bug): Browser-pane WebContentsView composited above the DOM Settings modal. *Fix:* Superseded: Settings became a native menu, modal deleted.
- **BUG-154** (bug): The setting-gated Return remap checked tab.kind==='claude', so claude typed into a shell tab was missed. *Fix:* Also gate on the ClaudePresenceProbe.
- **BUG-155** (bug): tiptap-markdown rewrote bare URLs into links, so the serialized text differed from disk and the conflict dialog fired. *Fix:* Autolink collapse added to normalizeForEchoCompare.
- **BUG-156** (bug): A transient 0x0 fit during a layout change resized the PTY to zero and killed Claude. *Fix:* Floor of 8 cols and 1 row at the PTY boundary, plus no /rename injection.
- **BUG-157** (followup): Sibling ResizeObserver-to-IPC patterns (image view, browser and aux-pane bounds to WebContentsView) could write bad sizes, as in BUG-156. *Fix:* Unfixed; filed as an audit task the owner could pick up.
- **BUG-158** (bug): /tmp is a symlink to /private/tmp; Duo encoded the literal path so it never found Claude's -private-tmp-X session folder. *Fix:* encodeProjectDir now resolves symlinks before encoding.
- **BUG-159** (bug): Injecting '/rename' into Claude's PTY looked uncommitted, but the JSONL showed it committed. A TUI rendering detail. *Fix:* Terminator changed to CR; T3 auto-hydrator turned off.
- **BUG-160** (bug): Clicking Resume on the S3 banner set dismissedBanner, which suppressed the S2 banner too. *Fix:* dismissedBanner scoped to the S3 branch only.
- **BUG-161** (bug): Closing the last member tab left focus stuck in a project with no tab. *Fix:* Direct close paths auto-release focus; two-layer probe guard; pinned projects skip.
- **BUG-162** (bug): Git returned /private/tmp/X but browser tabs opened by duo open /tmp/X arrived as file:///tmp/X; string compare in browserTabMembership missed, so tab went to the wrong project. *Fix:* Try two candidate paths (raw + /private stripped or prepended) against sorted project roots.
- **BUG-163** (bug): CLI resolveProjectRef returned null when a name matched several projects, giving a misleading no-match error. *Fix:* Returns {ambiguous:[...]} and CLI says 'Ambiguous name X matches N projects'.
- **BUG-164** (bug): Duplicates in pins broke togglePin's indexOf+splice. *Fix:* normalizeProjectsFile dedupes pins.
- **BUG-165** (bug): node-pty spawned into a deleted cwd, so the child exited at once. Every relaunch hit the same dead path. *Fix:* resolveExistingCwd walks up to the nearest surviving ancestor, then home, then /, with an amber in-stream note.
- **BUG-166** (bug): One baseline served both the dirty check and the conflict check, so any lossy round-trip raised a false conflict. *Fix:* Shared useDiskReconciliation hook; conflict baseline = raw disk bytes.
- **BUG-167** (bug): Persisted expanded folders and cwd were never pruned; each project switch re-listed deleted dirs and logged ENOENT, plus leftover debug focus logging. *Fix:* Prune dead expanded paths on mount; remove instrumentation.
- **BUG-186** (feature): Optional line gutter showed block counters rather than the source lines Claude Code's Read reports. *Fix:* Computed true source lines through the save path.
- **BUG-189** (bug): During quit, async IPC sinks in the Electron main process sent to windows that were destroyed or null. The id BUG-189 also collided with ENH-189 in the shared number space and was renumbered. *Fix:* A safeSend helper that guards against destroyed or null windows and window swaps. The bug was renumbered to BUG-190.
- **BUG-190** (bug): pty data during shutdown hit webContents.send on a destroyed window, looping an error dialog on quit. *Fix:* Guarded all async main-to-renderer sends (PR #61).
- **BUG-191** (bug): Tile membership came from a terminal's launch cwd, never updated when the shell cd'd away or exited, so tiles lingered. *Fix:* Membership follows live shell cwd via polled lsof, drops exited shells.
- **BUG-192** (bug): handleCloseProject re-entered through nested setState and a confirm dialog taken after state snapshot. *Fix:* Re-entrancy guard, confirm before snapshot, hoisted setActiveTabId.
- **BUG-193** (bug): Pinned tabs resolved up to a git-repo parent like ~/Documents, spawning a phantom tile and D11 auto-switch stealing focus. *Fix:* Pinned tabs get null project membership.
- **BUG-194** (bug): Terminal membership followed the live cwd, so a cd out of the focused project left focus on a project with no visible terminal. *Fix:* Release focus to All when the focused project leaves the rail.
- **BUG-195** (bug): Stale reference after reload left a ghost view composited over the UI. *Fix:* Close/promote reconcile unconditionally.
- **BUG-196** (bug): duo eval location.reload() on the aux WCV was followed by the dev app dying; seen once, unconfirmed. *Fix:* Unfixed, needs repro.
- **BUG-197** (bug): A collapsed navigator rail peeked open on hover; clicking whitespace committed it but a file/folder row click did not. *Fix:* Unfixed (filed on the v0.9.1 walk).
- **BUG-198** (bug): A base64 round trip exceeded the 10s client timeout and no file was written. *Fix:* Unfixed in legacy (queued).
- **BUG-199** (bug): Edit via ProseMirror reserialised and churned untouched markdown (83/77 lines for an 8-line insert). *Fix:* Filed; the fix direction was to splice in text space.
- **BUG-200** (bug): Collapsing the pane unmounted the React terminal tree, and each unmount cleanup killed its PTY (P0 data loss). *Fix:* Hide with display:none instead of unmounting, plus a TERMINAL_MIN_COLS floor.
- **BUG-201** (bug): Secondary window flipped dark on remount when macOS went dark although theme was pinned light. *Fix:* Unfixed (filed).
- **BUG-202** (bug): A load race showed the empty-state for a 35-line file. *Fix:* Unfixed; the filing suggested a defensive re-read or a loading state.
- **BUG-203** (bug): {++ ++} and {-- --} tokens did not decorate after remount. *Fix:* Unfixed; a parser fix was proposed.
- **BUG-204** (bug): Comparing nav cwd string with git's resolved workTreeRoot failed for symlinked paths, so no ribbon (and no worktree dropdown). *Fix:* Filed, unfixed; proposed realpath on both sides.
- **BUG-205** (bug): The tooltip source (pendingCwd) and the real spawn cwd diverged. *Fix:* Unfixed (P3 backlog).
- **BUG-206** (bug): Rollup folded by path containment, so ../repo-wt sibling worktrees became separate project cards while nested .claude/worktrees folded under the repo. *Fix:* Unfixed (filed P2 from the ENH-210 worktree-UI walk); direction: fold by git worktree identity (mainWorktreeRoot).
- **BUG-207** (bug): Legacy editor wrote a .md.duo.json sidecar next to every note (UI state like metadata-panel collapsed), cluttering git and Obsidian. *Fix:* Moved UI prefs to per-path localStorage with a one-time sidecar read fallback.
- **BUG-208** (bug): A hard-coded GROUP_ORDER list hid new verb families from help. *Fix:* Added vault/graph/base to the list.
- **BUG-209** (bug): Aux WebContentsView sat above DOM modals; the park fix covered only the main browser pane. *Fix:* Aux view parked/muted when a modal opens (#104).
- **BUG-210** (bug): The serializer emitted blank lines inside cells, which broke GFM rows (data loss). *Fix:* <br>-joined cell serializer plus a save-path backstop refusing row loss.
- **BUG-211** (bug): First click on a page's Copy button after the Electron WebContentsView lost keyboard focus did nothing, because Document.hasFocus() was false; second click worked. *Fix:* Unfixed (P3); direction was to keep the WCV focused on tab-activate.
- **BUG-212** (bug): Two things filed under one id: signed bundle lacked com.apple.security.device.gpu so processes under Duo's terminals (qmd embed etc.) fell back to CPU; and [[ autocomplete had no default-vault fallback. *Fix:* Added the gpu entitlement to build/entitlements.mac.plist with entitlementsInherit (Electron helpers). No before/after proof recorded (tasks-archive.md:197-203). Vault half not relevant.
- **BUG-213** (bug): ~/.claude/duo/hooks/ got deleted but settings.json still registered the scripts, so every Claude session in every project printed hook errors; installer only re-ran on version bump. *Fix:* Unfixed at filing (options: boot-time probe or doctor check).
- **BUG-214** (bug): External change to the default-vault pref (CLI, hand edit) did not refresh the Vault/Rollups tabs. *Fix:* Main process now notifies every window on pref change.
- **BUG-252** (bug): A hand-edited status panel in docs/roadmap.html went stale for 20+ releases. *Fix:* Removed the panel.
- **BUG-253** (bug): A defect in renaming a rollup-editor title. It was renumbered to BUG-256 after PR #121 took ENH-253 (a shared-id collision). *Fix:* Fixed as BUG-256 (PR #124).
- **BUG-254** (bug): Typing [[name, Enter, Enter quickly: second Enter swallowed while the async type registry loaded, so entity was mistyped or misfiled. *Fix:* Queued confirm fires on registry load; failures drop it; IME Enter ignored.
- **BUG-256** (bug): Editing a rollup's Title changed frontmatter title but left the filename unchanged. *Fix:* Shared saveRollupNoteWithRename with Enter-commit and collision rejection.
- **BUG-260** (bug): Unquoted filter expressions containing ': ' parsed as YAML maps, silently dropping the filter and demoting the note to view-only. *Fix:* Quote/emit YAML-safe filter lines and fold punctuation on both sides of name matching.
- **BUG-260b** (bug): targetKey kept punctuation while slugStem dropped it, so filter operands written as display names never matched. *Fix:* probeKeys folds both ways at every probe site.
- **BUG-265** (bug): A second instance's shutdown removed the first's socket and port files. *Fix:* Only unlink files that instance owns.
- **BUG-267** (bug): Two effects that follow the active surface fought each other: a project switch moved focus to a tab in another project, which switched the project back. The fix sat unmerged on PR #137 and the bug was rediscovered as BUG-269. *Fix:* A pure adjudicateActiveSurfaceFocusSwitch gate. The fix stayed unmerged for two months, which shows a fix left on an open PR can be lost.
- **BUG-269** (bug): D11 auto-switch and keep-visible effect each corrected focusedProject vs activeWorking in opposite directions forever. *Fix:* Single adjudication of activations; focusing a project with no terminal shows a placeholder, no auto-spawn.
- **BUG-270** (bug): Stale WebContentsView bounds survived a renderer reload and covered the editor. *Fix:* Main re-syncs bounds on host renderer reload; split-view resize works for browser aux.
- **BUG-271** (bug): A [[ or @ autocomplete left open in a tab that went inactive never closed. Its popover was portaled to document.body and re-anchored to a zero rect at the window's top-left over every other tab. *Fix:* Shipped 2026-09-18 (PR #140). TipTap Suggestion only closes from its own editor's transactions, and hidden editors get none. Fixed with one shared suspend/resume lifecycle plus a guard that refuses to render against an anchor that isn't laid out.
- **BUG-272** (bug): An expected-missing sidecar read went through a throwing IPC handler, which spammed errors. *Fix:* Unfixed; a non-throwing read was proposed.
- **BUG-273** (bug): Type-picker popover anchored to a snapshotted rect stayed over other tabs after a non-mouse tab switch (duo edit, quick switcher). *Fix:* Cancel the picker when its host tab hides.
- **BUG-274** (bug): Under iCloud-synced ~/Documents macOS evicted git packfiles and tracked files, breaking commits and hanging the dev app's main process in uninterruptible IO. *Fix:* Developer-only: improve materialize.sh, scan packfiles in check-materialization.sh, make doctor say 'app running but not answering'.
- **BUG-275** (bug): A vitest live-git test hit the 5 s default timeout when the whole suite ran, then passed on its own. *Fix:* Unfixed; the suggestion was a longer timeout for describe blocks that run live subprocesses.
- **ENH-001** (feature): duo html new stamped ULID data-duo-id on elements so the 'Add stable IDs?' prompt never showed. *Fix:* shipped v0.3.1
- **ENH-002** (feature): Cmd-Shift-V and a Paste and Match Style menu item, inserting text/plain only. *Fix:* Shipped in v0.3.1 plus a menu item.
- **ENH-003** (feature): Install bootstrapped pins.json with FAQ and What Duo Does help pages pre-pinned in the browser pane. *Fix:* Shipped, then FAQ retired entirely (ENH-135).
- **ENH-004** (feature): New canvas got an Atelier-style inline stylesheet, dark mode and viewport meta. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-005** (feature): Hover-reveal copy button on each code block in the markdown editor and canvas. *Fix:* Shipped as ProseMirror decorations.
