- **ENH-006** (feature): A discrete secondary button to open a browser tab, replacing option-click. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-007** (feature): Canvas comment rail collapsed to a pill when all threads resolved, so resolved comments stayed findable. *Fix:* shipped v0.5.1
- **ENH-008** (feature): Explanatory hover tooltips on the dual-pane navigator headers. *Fix:* shipped (title attributes)
- **ENH-009** (feature): Seed Slack, Gmail, Google Workspace, Atlassian, M365 into the list of hosts bounced to the system browser. *Fix:* shipped v0.4.3
- **ENH-010** (feature): A Pinned section at the bottom of the navigator for cross-folder frequent targets, grouped by parent, stored in nav-pins.json with CLI pin/unpin/pins. *Fix:* shipped v0.5.0
- **ENH-011** (feature): Welcome and update banner text rewritten to drop jargon (skill, subagent, hook). *Fix:* Shipped.
- **ENH-012** (feature): First-install default for the user-settings navigator pane flipped to collapsed to give the project tree room. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-013** (feature): Send to Duo pill gated by a PTY process probe so selection never pasted into a bare shell. *Fix:* shipped v0.5.1
- **ENH-014** (feature): View > Pane size submenu with accelerators and duo split <pct\|preset> CLI. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-015** (bug): The Files column collapse button was text-zinc-600 on cream and used an unconventional glyph; the owner could not find it. *Fix:* Shipped: colour bumped to text-ink-mute and a Finder-style sidebar-toggle glyph. A first-launch coach-mark was deferred.
- **ENH-016** (feature): There was no way to create a file or folder in the tree from the right-click menu. The v1 used window.prompt(), which is disabled in Electron and silently did nothing. Right-click on empty space showed no menu (BUG-041). *Fix:* Hotfix: create untitled.md or untitled-folder at once and drop the row into rename mode. The whitespace menu was tracked separately.
- **ENH-017** (feature): After linking ~/.local/bin/duo, the banner offered a one-click fenced PATH block appended to .zshrc/.bash_profile/fish config. *Fix:* Shipped, idempotent.
- **ENH-018** (feature): Preserve * versus - markers on a TipTap round-trip, and render disc versus dash accordingly. *Fix:* A BulletListWithMarker attribute and a serialize override.
- **ENH-019** (feature): Hide horizontal scrollbars on overflowing tab strips. *Fix:* shipped (scrollbar-none)
- **ENH-020** (feature): Skill guidance on canvas templates, ID conventions and agent-event buttons. *Fix:* Shipped as skill content.
- **ENH-021** (feature): Upgrade appended new bundled default domains to a user's existing file. *Fix:* shipped v0.5.3
- **ENH-022** (feature): Agent-driven editor scroll to a heading, line or slug anchor; the heading mode was flaky in legacy. *Fix:* Shipped, with the heading mode left buggy.
- **ENH-023** (feature): Find in the markdown editor, plus a read-only doc find verb. *Fix:* Shipped.
- **ENH-024** (feature): scrollIntoView on active tab change for both strips when tabs exceed width. *Fix:* shipped v0.5.3
- **ENH-025** (feature): Bound sinkListItem/liftListItem to Cmd-] and Cmd-[. *Fix:* Shipped in v0.5.3.
- **ENH-026** (feature): Tab context menu with Reveal in navigator, Rename, Delete for file tabs. *Fix:* shipped v0.5.3
- **ENH-027** (feature): Meta tag to route local HTML to the editable canvas or browser. *Fix:* superseded by verb-driven modality (ENH-156)
- **ENH-028** (feature): Find bar in the browser pane via findInPage; before it, Cmd-F did nothing there. *Fix:* shipped v0.5.4
- **ENH-029** (bug): The navigator breadcrumb defaulted to scrolled left, so a deep path showed ~/Documents/... and hid the active folder. *Fix:* Shipped: scrollLeft = scrollWidth on every cwd change, last segment semibold.
- **ENH-030** (feature): Context-menu entry and Cmd-Option-C stripping formatting marks on copy. *Fix:* Shipped.
- **ENH-031** (bug): Electron renderers showed no context menu in editor, canvas or browser pane (no Cut/Copy/Paste/spell/Inspect). *Fix:* Shipped v0.5.4 via electron-context-menu attached to every webContents.
- **ENH-032** (feature): With conda (base) setting LC_ALL=C in the user's shell, multi-byte paste rendered as raw bytes; added doctor locale section and FAQ entry. *Fix:* duo doctor probes LC_ALL/LC_CTYPE/LANG and flags non-UTF-8 (shipped v0.5.5).
- **ENH-033** (feature): Titlebar badge '0.5.4 dev' left of theme toggle, to confirm which build is live. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-034** (feature): Canvas could open read-only or editable by meta; pencil/eye toggle. *Fix:* shipped v0.6.0
- **ENH-035** (feature): Right-clicking a working-pane tab had no Copy path; closed as duplicate of ENH-074. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3 via ENH-074.
- **ENH-036** (feature): The new browser tab became the active working tab. *Fix:* Focus handler flips activeWorking.
- **ENH-037** (bug): The Window menu's close role kept Cmd-W, so Cmd-W on a tab closed the window and lost about 20 minutes of unsaved page form notes (data loss). *Fix:* Window close moved to Cmd-Shift-W; plain Cmd-W reserved for closing the tab.
- **ENH-038** (feature): The walk worksheet saved textarea and radio state to localStorage and restored it on reload. It had a Clear saved walk button. *Fix:* Shipped.
- **ENH-039** (feature): Paths in the walk's step text became links, routed to Duo through a CDP forwarder. *Fix:* Shipped.
- **ENH-040** (feature): Titlebar toggle buttons hide the terminal or the canvas completely and restore it to the last drag-set width. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3 with a prevSplitPct cache.
- **ENH-041** (feature): The right pane could host two files side by side (an aux slot), via the CLI, a menu or a chord. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.4-5.
- **ENH-042** (feature): Drag tabs horizontally, or use Move Tab Left/Right; the pinned zone is preserved. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3.
- **ENH-043** (feature): The worksheet fired item-changed events that `duo events --follow` consumed, so Claude saw verdicts live. Copy and paste was the offline fallback. *Fix:* Shipped (reframed as a meta-tracker for playground primitives).
- **ENH-044** (feature): Replaced the generic + on the new-Claude half of the split button with the Clawd mascot SVG. *Fix:* Shipped.
- **ENH-045** (feature): A parent ENH for the navigator's Project Claude Context section: collapsible, named from the project, projects detected from .claude or a git root, GitHub status and actions, promote to project, sync to GitHub, and a new-project skill. *Fix:* Only 045a shipped. b, c and d (gh status, promote and sync, new-project skill) stayed queued.
- **ENH-045a** (feature): The navigator's Project Claude Context section became collapsible (default collapsed) and was titled from package.json name or the folder name. *Fix:* Shipped in v0.6.3 (ProjectClaudeContext.tsx).
- **ENH-046** (feature): Smoke-walk page and canvas templates rendered commands as plain text; owner wanted pre blocks with a Copy button. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3: commands render in pre blocks with Copy buttons; contract documented in make-page.md.
- **ENH-047** (feature): A walk step told the user to paste 'duo events --follow' into a terminal; the owner said a walk cannot rely on pasted commands. *Fix:* Unfixed (filed). Recommended spawning the listener via 'duo new-tab --cmd'.
- **ENH-048** (feature): One walk step (events cursor) did not say which terminal to use or whether the V8 listener should still be running. *Fix:* Unfixed (filed). Wanted a rewrite of the step with an explicit terminal hand-off.
- **ENH-049** (feature): Lesson pack canvas fired claude:spawn with prose cmd that got typed into zsh; fixed to launch claude then send cmd as first message. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.1: send 'claude' then cmd.
- **ENH-050** (bug): The web content view hid in-renderer menus and confirm modals (BUG-058/064); they were replaced with a native NSMenu and system sheets. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3.
- **ENH-051** (feature): fork.config.json packs.disabled lets enterprise distros suppress default packs. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-052** (followup): Internal identifiers used 'canvas' while the product called them pages/playgrounds. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.5: 177 edits across 32 files, no behaviour change.
- **ENH-053** (feature): Copy-and-customize lesson template and a lesson-runtime skill for authoring playground lessons. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-054** (feature): Skill recognition so a user asking Claude for a training gets a lesson built, instead of a CLI verb. *Fix:* shipped as auto-loaded make-playground skill
- **ENH-055** (feature): duo html click plus a skill to walk every step of a lesson for smoke testing. *Fix:* shipped (click verb, flythrough skill)
- **ENH-056** (feature): Template for multi-module lesson curricula. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-066** (feature): A collapsed pane became a 36px clickable rail, with a glyph and a rotated label, to restore it. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3.
- **ENH-067** (feature): The curated ~/.claude pane at the top of the navigator listed CLAUDE.md, skills and agents but not Duo's own ~/.claude/duo/ folder. *Fix:* Shipped: 'duo' added to the curated entry list.
- **ENH-068** (feature): The > chevron on the new-browser-tab button read poorly; changed to a globe. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-069** (feature): Sticky toggle in the editor showing a gutter of line numbers (v1 numbered blocks, not wrapped lines), persisted in localStorage. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3 as CSS counters on ProseMirror blocks; later true source line numbers (BUG-186).
- **ENH-070** (feature): The installer copied repo help/*.html into ~/.claude/duo/help, so the installed FAQ drifted from source in dev. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.4: in dev (unpackaged) the installer symlinks to source and only skips files the user customized. Production unchanged.
- **ENH-071** (followup): Cosmetic: toggle text 'Lines' changed to '#'. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3.
- **ENH-072** (feature): The rail label went from 11px to 13px for legibility. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3.
- **ENH-073** (feature): A hairline divider between the scrolling tabs and the sticky new-tab buttons. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3.
- **ENH-074** (feature): Add Copy path to the tab right-click menu. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.3.
- **ENH-075** (feature): Design exploration of the canvas icon; owner found none better. *Fix:* closed, no change
- **ENH-076** (feature): Indent/outdent shortcuts in the editable HTML canvas, parity with markdown editor. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-077** (bug): Dev builds showed Electron's default icon in dialog.showMessageBox; production was already correct. *Fix:* Verified the code path; a DMG check was still owed.
- **ENH-078** (bug): The selected row was barely visible. Clicking whitespace or re-clicking the selected row did not clear the selection, so the 'new file here' target could not go back to the root. *Fix:* Heavier accent fill and weight, plus three deselect paths: re-click the row, click whitespace, Esc.
- **ENH-079** (feature): When the navigator collapsed to a rail it showed only a folder glyph. The owner wanted a vertical project-name label like the terminal and canvas rails. *Fix:* Shipped: a vertical italic label in the collapsed rail.
- **ENH-080** (feature): A quick switcher across open file and browser tabs (Cmd-Shift-A). *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.8 as a renderer overlay.
- **ENH-081** (feature): Added CFBundleDocumentTypes for md/html and an open-file handler routing to editor/browser (cold start queued). *Fix:* Shipped (v0.6.4).
- **ENH-082** (feature): A collapsible strip under the terminal tabs where the user and agent state the terminal's job, working docs and skills. *Fix:* Filed, with a research doc owed; never built.
- **ENH-083** (feature): The collapse buttons moved from the titlebar to sit next to the pane's new-tab cluster. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.5.
- **ENH-084** (bug): The active-pane glow on the split aux pane could not be made reliable; three fixes failed because DOM focus signals were inconsistent across WebContentsViews. *Fix:* Deferred with instrumentation; unfixed.
- **ENH-085** (feature): The Split View aux header got the same context menu as the main tab (Reveal, Rename, Copy path, Trash). *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.5.
- **ENH-086** (feature): The ~/.claude pane and the project tree were visually too close together. The fix ended with the user-claude pane moved to the bottom under a thick rule. *Fix:* Shipped (v2 of the fix): reordered to the bottom with a border-t-2 and a tinted background.
- **ENH-087** (feature): Open files were bold in the navigator, which users could not decode. A small dot marked 'open in a tab' and the active tab got an accent dot. *Fix:* Shipped: a 6px ink-mute dot for open-but-inactive files, and the accent dot for the active one.
- **ENH-088** (feature): Installer wrote a versioned managed block into CLAUDE.md so non-Duo Claude sessions know about Duo, hook-independent; sticky flag remembers user removal. *Fix:* Shipped (v0.6.6).
- **ENH-089** (feature): A user-facing glossary (page/playground/lesson/canvas) moved out of the project CLAUDE.md into a skill reference. *Fix:* Shipped skill/references/vocabulary.md.
- **ENH-090** (feature): A doc on what works when hooks or permissions are locked down by policy. *Fix:* Shipped skill reference.
- **ENH-091** (feature): Typing in a new canvas landed in the H1; seeding the caret did not stick. Deferred indefinitely. *Fix:* unfixed
- **ENH-092** (feature): state:save/restore and data-bind-* runtime primitives for Claude-authored interactive pages. *Fix:* shipped, then owner pushed back that primitives are too restrictive
- **ENH-093** (feature): compose:result/json, clipboard:copy, host:send-to-claude actions for pages. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-094** (feature): CDP injection so data-duo-action buttons in browser tabs emitted events Claude could follow. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-095** (feature): Two redundant buttons on the aux header merged into one Move back to main. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.7.
- **ENH-096** (feature): [[ autocomplete, cmd+click wikilink navigation (vault root found via .obsidian/), cmd+O vault quick switcher; several walks to fix. *Fix:* Partly shipped; root cause of the stubborn cmd+click bug was exists() returning false for directories.
- **ENH-097** (feature): Override to force canvas edit mode on a page whose meta says browser; right-click Edit in canvas. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-098** (feature): Cmd-Shift-L, Cmd-Shift-; and Cmd-Shift-' jump focus to the terminal, main canvas or aux, with CLI parity; legacy's Cmd-Opt chords were intercepted by window managers. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 9 with a visible-editor filter.
- **ENH-099** (feature): A chord, menu item and CLI verb that reset the panes to 33/33/33. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.11.
- **ENH-100** (feature): Right-click verb to toggle editability of a file (HTML via duo-default-editable meta); draft only, never refined. *Fix:* unfixed (DRAFT).
- **ENH-101** (feature): Proposed chords to make a pane full-screen; closed won't-fix as redundant with Cmd-Opt-0/9. *Fix:* Closed won't fix.
- **ENH-102** (feature): A chord to move the active file tab's file to the Trash after a confirm. It did nothing for browser or terminal tabs, and treated ENOENT as success. *Fix:* Shipped: ⌘⇧⌫ runs the same flow as right-click, Move to Trash, then closes the tab.
- **ENH-103** (feature): Merged duplicate 'Saved' text and Save button into one four-state pill (Saved, Save, Saving, Failed-retry). *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 10 as SaveControl.
- **ENH-104** (feature): Hover-reveal Autosave on/off toggle; off suppresses only the debounce, Cmd-S still writes. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 10.
- **ENH-105** (feature): Typing @ in the TipTap canvas editor opened a filename popover and inserted a [[wikilink]]; mid-word @ (emails) rejected. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 11 as AtMention TipTap extension with custom matcher.
- **ENH-106** (feature): Mark a .md read-only via YAML frontmatter so a regenerated mirror (e.g. a Notion-synced file) cannot take local edits that are later overwritten. *Fix:* unfixed (DRAFT).
- **ENH-107** (feature): Right-click a terminal tab to Move left or right, with a CLI verb. *Fix:* Draft, unbuilt.
- **ENH-108** (feature): Cmd-V or drop of an image saved image-<stamp>-<hash>.<ext> next to the doc and inserted an inline reference; untitled buffers declined. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 12.
- **ENH-109** (feature): Legacy added .obsidian to the file tree's always-visible dotdir list (like .claude) so vault config could be edited. *Fix:* shipped Sprint 11 (always-visible exception in FileTree shouldShow)
- **ENH-110** (feature): New kind:'json' canvas tab to view/edit API responses, configs, webhook payloads for the PM persona. *Fix:* Shipped as a JSON/YAML viewer tab.
- **ENH-111** (feature): Image viewer toolbar (zoom, fit, 1:1, copy image, size readout, context menu, pan), plus planned CSV/YAML/Mermaid viewers. *Fix:* Image viewer v2 shipped; broken-icon bugs fixed via duo-asset:// protocol, CSP and blob URLs.
- **ENH-112** (feature): Repo-only guided tutorial and skill for building distro packs. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-113** (bug): When a file open in a tab was removed from disk, the buffer stayed, and autosave failed silently or recreated the file. *Fix:* Filed only, with a plan: close a clean tab, banner a dirty one. Not shipped.
- **ENH-114** (feature): Obsidian parity: cmd+click a nonexistent [[Page]] creates it at vault root (mkdir for path forms). *Fix:* shipped Sprint 10
- **ENH-115** (feature): Jumps the file tree to the terminal tab's cwd. *Fix:* Shipped.
- **ENH-116** (followup): The smoke-walk skill reached 603 lines, so rules at the end were silently dropped when Claude loaded it. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.11: trimmed to 241 lines, with detail moved to four reference docs under references/.
- **ENH-117** (feature): Cmd-Opt-V showed the raw source in place of the rendered editor/canvas (read-only panel). *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.12, panel-fill.
- **ENH-118** (followup): Decision record: GIFs animate, SVG stays inert via <img>, HEIC and PDF handled by ENH-128/129. *Fix:* Decisions captured, no code.
- **ENH-119** (bug): Images inside a selected range showed no selection feedback, so cut/copy/delete outcome was unclear. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 14 with a PM decoration class.
- **ENH-120** (feature): Cmd-C over text+image gave only text (the markdown ![](...)) in other apps. *Fix:* Shipped as known limitation then polish, writing image bytes alongside text.
- **ENH-121** (feature): Renderer console output was invisible to the dev process, which cost about 90 minutes diagnosing an image-render bug. *Fix:* Shipped: in dev mode, console-message events print to stdout as [renderer:level] (file:line), with Vite and security noise filtered.
- **ENH-122** (feature): A dev tool to query the Electron renderer's DOM. *Fix:* Shipped with duo devtools and duo layout.
- **ENH-123** (feature): Filed after the Sprint 12 retro: a CLI verb to open DevTools on the Electron renderer (or the browser pane, or close it), as a backstop for ENH-122's targeted DOM query. *Fix:* Shipped as a debug verb (Electron openDevTools one-liner).
- **ENH-124** (feature): JSON describing split, main/aux tab kinds and sizes, focused slot, terminal and navigator widths, because an agent misread a split layout from pixels. *Fix:* Shipped as duo layout.
- **ENH-125** (feature): Parity so duo image insert <path> worked against HTML canvas pages as well as the markdown editor; also fixed multiple mounted editors answering the IPC. *Fix:* Shipped with per-tab isActive gate.
- **ENH-126** (feature): Opening an aux pane snapped the panes to 33/33/33 (or 50/50 with the terminal collapsed). *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.11.
- **ENH-127** (feature): Remapped plain Return to ESC+CR in Claude tabs, with Cmd+Return to submit. It was reverted, re-shipped as a setting, then defaulted back. *Fix:* Shipped as an opt-in setting that sends ESC CR on Return.
- **ENH-128** (feature): HEIC from Photos.app failed to decode via nativeImage; fixed with a sips fallback to JPEG. *Fix:* Shipped v0.7.2 (sips fallback).
- **ENH-129** (feature): Pasting or dropping a PDF saved it beside the doc and inserted [name.pdf](path) at the drop point. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 14 (drop uses posAtCoords).
- **ENH-130** (feature): When the agent makes a playground/HTML/markdown file, Duo should expand the canvas, open it and focus it (duo open/edit --reveal); playgrounds get Send to Claude and Copy buttons. *Fix:* Shipped as --reveal on duo edit/open/view that auto-expands the working pane.
- **ENH-131** (feature): Right-click an HTML canvas tab to close it and reopen the same file as a browser tab so scripts run. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 14: menu entry mirroring Edit in canvas.
- **ENH-132** (feature): Tabs were plain buttons; added role=tab, tablist and aria-selected so VoiceOver announces them as tabs. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 14.
- **ENH-133** (feature): Shift+Enter in a Claude tab was mapped to the bytes ESC CR (what Option+Enter sends) so that it inserts a newline. *Fix:* Shipped by widening the ENH-127 key-handler condition.
- **ENH-134** (followup): Planning artifact deciding to ship default FTUX content as a built-in pack; closed. *Fix:* decisions captured 2026-05-10
- **ENH-135** (feature): help/faq.html retired from the install and default landing; moved into the repo docs. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.13.
- **ENH-136** (followup): Empty curriculum skeleton pack was removed from defaults and retired into a template. *Fix:* shipped (removed)
- **ENH-137** (feature): Owner-authored Beginner's Guide, polished by Claude, to ship as in-app pack/help content after the FAQ was removed; open in legacy (tasks.md:2739). *Fix:* unfixed (awaiting owner draft)
- **ENH-138** (feature): Installer op copies default HTML/markdown FTUX content from packs/duo-default/ at first launch, with an upgrade path. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-139** (feature): Extend distro-pack default kinds beyond 'canvas' to editor, markdown-preview, browser. Deferred until needed. *Fix:* unfixed (open/deferred)
- **ENH-140** (feature): Files retired between versions stayed in ~/.claude/duo/ forever; added SHA-map diff that deletes untouched orphans and keeps edited ones. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 16.
- **ENH-141** (bug): Installed CLI landed in dirs not on PATH inside Duo terminals; sandboxed Claude blocks dotfile writes, so duo was command-not-found for agents. *Fix:* Symlink into SHIM_DIR which Duo already prepends to PATH at PTY spawn; install also appended a PATH fence to .zshrc.
- **ENH-142** (feature): Legacy remapped plain Return to ESC+CR (newline) in Claude tabs, so a real submit needed Cmd+Return. ENH-142 made Return submit again by default and kept the remap as claudeReturn/shiftReturn prefs with CLI verbs. *Fix:* Shipped in Sprint 16 (v0.6.15). Return submits by default, Shift+Return writes ESC CR (a newline), and the override stays as a toggle.
- **ENH-143** (feature): The owner asked for a close-tab chord. It was resolved as the existing Cmd-W (close tab), with Cmd-Shift-Delete deleting the file, made easier to find. *Fix:* Shipped as a docs entry only.
- **ENH-144** (bug): Closing a file tab jumped focus to the far left (the browser tab) instead of the left neighbour. *Fix:* Pick the left neighbour, with the right neighbour as a fallback when the closed tab was leftmost.
- **ENH-145** (feature): Research request: delta inventory of Duo vault functionality vs native Obsidian client. *Fix:* unfixed (research request, low-medium)
- **ENH-146** (feature): A shared CSS kernel and class library so agents stop writing ~200 lines of CSS per playground. *Fix:* Shipped skill/references/duo-atelier.css and docs.
- **ENH-147** (feature): The tree could only select one row. ⌘-click toggled a non-contiguous selection, and the context menu offered 'Move N items to Trash', confirmed once. *Fix:* Shipped v1 in Sprint 17: selectedItems Map, a batch menu and external deletes pruning the selection.
- **ENH-148** (feature): The follow-on to ENH-147: ⇧-click range select across expanded folders, ⌘-A select all (capped at the cwd's children), and nav-state exposing selectedPaths. *Fix:* Shipped with the ENH-148 changelog line: range select, ⌘-A and selectedPaths in nav-state.
- **ENH-149** (feature): Research playground comparing ways to clone on a clean enterprise Mac (brew gh, pkg, bundled gh, GCM, SSH, plain HTTPS); closed with detect, validate, guide, optionally run. *Fix:* shipped (research); principle fed ENH-150.
- **ENH-150** (feature): Generic doctor/probe framework for pack integrations such as GitHub auth. *Fix:* unfixed/design (docs/research/integration-primitive-design.html)
- **ENH-150a** (feature): Sub-item of the integration primitive design doc (doctor/probe sub-slice). No tasks.md section; appears only in the design HTML. *Fix:* unfixed
- **ENH-150b** (feature): Second sub-item of the integration primitive design doc; no tasks.md section. *Fix:* unfixed
- **ENH-151** (feature): CLI verb and menu modal to clone a GitHub repo via gh, with auth probe. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-152** (feature): Root chip with branch and dirty/ahead/behind; owner rejected 'clean invisible' and wanted a visible repo-root indicator. *Fix:* v2 design: always-visible repo chip with popover.
- **ENH-152a** (feature): Branch icon on every repo-root folder row with hover popover (branch, dirty, ahead, behind); CLI duo git status and gh-auth. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-152b** (feature): Dirty dot per changed file in Navigator with a status/diff tooltip. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-152c** (feature): Navigator git status refreshed from file events (bounded depth, ignoring caches) instead of only on cwd change/focus. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-153** (feature): Sketch: on first launch, open the Doctor canvas tab or show a banner if a required distro-pack integration probes missing. Never built. *Fix:* unfixed (sketch, tied to distro packs and integrations).
- **ENH-154** (feature): Flow A: git init, commit, gh repo create --push. Flow B: add remote, fetch, conflict gate. Modal plus CLI. *Fix:* unfixed (filed, decisions pending)
- **ENH-155** (feature): Context menu items on files/folders to open or copy the GitHub URL; github.com and github.<company>.com. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-156** (feature): Two entries share the id. (1) Self-heal ~/.claude/duo/bin/duo on every boot with a log of failures. (2) duo open <html> goes to the browser, duo edit <html> to canvas. *Fix:* (1) ensureCliShim at app.whenReady, shipped as ENH-158. (2) shipped verb split.
- **ENH-157** (feature): duo html comment did not work on HTML mounted in the browser pane because only PageTab listened; fix was a CDP-injected sidecar overlay. *Fix:* Filed Sprint 18 as a follow-up to ENH-156; not shown as shipped.
- **ENH-158** (feature): Shipped form of the ENH-156 self-heal: SHIM_DIR/duo rebuilt automatically each launch. *Fix:* Shipped in PR #52.
- **ENH-159b** (feature): Toggle hover-outline element picker in the browser pane; click sends a structured snapshot (tag, selector path, heading trail, text, attrs) to the active terminal; ESC exits. *Fix:* Shipped (only mentioned in CLI-COVERAGE.md).
- **ENH-159** (feature): Inspect mode (hover outline, click to freeze, send) with element selector, heading trail and text in the payload; Cmd-Shift-C / duo inspect. *Fix:* Shipped v0.7.0 as a three-state freeze machine with an anchored pill.
- **ENH-160** (feature): Script wrapping pkgbuild/productbuild to bundle Duo plus an internal distro pack for enterprise distribution. *Fix:* Shipped (unsigned pkg).
- **ENH-162** (bug): Cloning into an existing folder failed with cryptic gh/git stderr; fixed with pre-flight collision check, amber warning, Reveal in Finder, friendlier error. v2 deferred: detect existing clone of the same URL and offer Open. *Fix:* shipped pre-flight check + friendly error (v1); match-existing-repo detection deferred.
- **ENH-163** (feature): Pill label changed from Send to Duo to Send to agent in three implementations. *Fix:* Label rename shipped.
- **ENH-164** (feature): Closed as already shipped: duo new-tab --claude --cwd existed; the gap was discoverability. *Fix:* Memory rule updated to cite the verb; lesson: grep coverage doc before filing.
- **ENH-165** (followup): The owner wanted a one-time style spec for annotated doc screenshots, chosen through a playground. *Fix:* Unfixed (filed). Style choice pending.
- **ENH-166** (feature): One rail interleaving comments and tracked changes by document position, replacing two stacked rails. *Fix:* Shipped v2 of the rail.
- **ENH-167** (feature): Round-trip open tabs, terminals and browser state to a user-saved file, with File menu items and duo workspace CLI. *Fix:* shipped (later defeatured)
- **ENH-168** (feature): Design study of five switcher positions with locked owner decisions (title-bar dropdown). *Fix:* shipped as study; implemented by ENH-171
- **ENH-169** (feature): Three triggers for creating a file or folder in the navigator, with a shared default-location rule: a breadcrumb right-click menu, File menu entries, and the chords ⌘N and ⌘⇧N. *Fix:* Shipped in Sprint 20 / v0.7.7.
- **ENH-170** (feature): A top-level Settings menu with one checkbox for the Cmd+Return submit override. It was the native replacement for a modal that had an occlusion bug (BUG-153). *Fix:* Shipped as a native menu item, and the modal was deleted.
- **ENH-171** (feature): Click the title-bar workspace badge for recent workspaces, New Workspace, Clear Recent. *Fix:* shipped v0.7.7
- **ENH-172** (feature): A View menu checkbox, ⌘⇧., a duo hidden-files verb and a showDotfiles field in nav-state. .claude and .obsidian stayed visible. *Fix:* Shipped (600d16e).
- **ENH-173** (bug): duo view on a folder opened a canvas tab showing a no-preview fallback with only Open with default app, a dead end. *Fix:* Added a primary Navigate here button.
- **ENH-174** (bug): TipTap auto-converted prd.md or example.com to links, changing the markdown on round trip and causing false 'changed on disk' conflicts. *Fix:* Set autolink:false in Link.configure; BUG-155 also normalised echo compare.
- **ENH-175** (bug): An agent navigating the active browser tab clobbered the page the owner was reading. *Fix:* navigateOrFocus: new tab or focus an existing match (hash and trailing slash stripped); address bar keeps reuse-active.
- **ENH-176** (feature): Two localStorage flags let the Send pill target the Claude agent (default) or a plain terminal when no Claude is live. *Fix:* Shipped v0.7.7.
- **ENH-177** (feature): Banner with one-click Resume on a restored terminal tab that hosted Claude; scraped latest JSONL for the id. *Fix:* Built, reverted, folded into ENH-183 as S3, later pared to a restore offer.
- **ENH-178** (feature): Default local-only: only file/localhost render in Duo, everything else pops the system browser; filtered and unfiltered modes optional. *Fix:* Re-shipped v0.7.8.
- **ENH-179** (feature): A last-in-first-out ring of 10 closed tabs (file, browser, terminal). Cmd-Z restored the last one unless focus was in a text input. *Fix:* Shipped v0.7.7 with an inAnyTextInput gate.
- **ENH-180** (feature): Inject /rename into the PTY; closed same day because Claude writes Haiku summaries itself. *Fix:* Closed
- **ENH-181** (feature): Inline rename via PTY /rename, collapsed marker, tap to expand. *Fix:* Folded into ENH-183, then pared away
- **ENH-182** (feature): Auto-derived projects, rail tiles, focus filter, pins and colours, stale-reference self-heal, duo project CLI. *Fix:* shipped v0.7.10 to v0.8.0
- **ENH-183** (feature): Read ladder customTitle > aiTitle > firstPrompt > uuid, S1 resume pills for a fresh tab, S3 restore offer, session list/resume CLI; S2/force-rename pared out. *Fix:* Pared to S1, S3, ladder, BUG-158, BUG-160 in v0.7.9
- **ENH-184** (feature): Legacy top-bar workspace pill was made a passive label (menu behind a flag) and the '+ New Workspace' handler routing was fixed, with a CLI parity verb. *Fix:* Shipped v0.8.0: pill defeatured, flag hook, `duo workspace-pill-menu` verb.
- **ENH-185** (feature): Rail 10% narrower and tooltip 'Project: {name}' instead of name+root. *Fix:* Shipped v0.8.0 (cosmetic).
- **ENH-186** (bug): Rail tiles used name.slice(0,2), giving a stack of identical 'AI' tiles. *Fix:* computeProjectAbbreviations helper: initials of first two words, extend on collision, numeric suffix fallback.
- **ENH-187** (feature): Cmd+T and duo new-tab used the navigator's launch cwd instead of the focused tab's live shell cwd. They also raced with follow-mode after a quick tab switch. *Fix:* Fixed in v0.8.1. A new PTY_LIVE_CWD IPC (lsof) asks the focused tab for its cwd before spawning, and falls back to the launch cwd and then pendingCwd.
- **ENH-188** (feature): Right-clicking a terminal tab offered only Reveal. ENH-188 added Move left/right, Copy cwd, Close tab, Close other tabs and drag-reorder. *Fix:* Built on a branch (PR #60) with a new tab-id dataTransfer type.
- **ENH-189** (feature): A research playground mapping which Duo touchpoints work, do nothing or break under OpenAI's Codex CLI. It found the socket bridge is mostly harness-neutral and the coupling sits in the lifecycle layer. *Fix:* Research only. The decision cards were never walked.
- **ENH-190** (feature): The navigator had two fixed widths. This added a drag border that eases back, a collapsed-rail hover-peek, a drag-past-96px collapse and hover grip pills. *Fix:* Shipped v0.9.1 after a prototype and locked tuning.
- **ENH-191** (followup): A 10-agent audit found systemic version drift in docs (footers frozen at old versions) and a 1.2 MB tasks.md. The id was also reused for the multi-window feature in the changelog. *Fix:* Decisions D1-D9 shipped (archive split, version sourced from package.json, hard-gate in cut-version). Screenshot steps D10/D11 remained.
- **ENH-195** (bug): External (CLI/agent) writes caused slow reloads, silent overwrites when autosave left the buffer clean, and false-positive conflict banners from normalisation. *Fix:* v0.9.0: one reconciliation primitive, raw-byte baseline, echo suppression, state machine (clean small reload, destructive options, dirty conflict).
- **ENH-196** (feature): Markdown editor highlights changed text on external reload but the HTML canvas did not; deferred because DOM diffing is fuzzy. *Fix:* Unfixed (deferred ENH).
- **ENH-197** (feature): A clean buffer silently replaced by a >50% external rewrite offered only a strip; added Keep mine / Load new / View diff (diff as tracked changes). *Fix:* Shipped v0.9.1.
- **ENH-198** (feature): Agents asked to 'use track changes' wrote literal <ins> tags, which did not render as accept/reject changes; wanted CLI guidance or auto-conversion to CriticMarkup. *Fix:* unfixed (filed).
- **ENH-199** (followup): After a lost-update race in pins/projects/session-state was fixed with a write queue, about six other services still used a fixed .duo.tmp and an unserialized read-modify-write. *Fix:* Filed only; not done.
- **ENH-200** (followup): ESLint ran only by hand, with no CI or pre-push hook. *Fix:* Unfixed (filed).
- **ENH-201** (feature): While dragging the navigator border toward collapse, the border turned red with a hint. The owner read red as an error for a non-destructive action. *Fix:* Filed only; not done.
- **ENH-202** (feature): Dirty-buffer conflict banner extended from 2 to 3 buttons with View diff (2-way, yours vs disk). *Fix:* Shipped v0.9.x.
- **ENH-203** (feature): 827-line skill had phantom verbs, dead links and drifted from the CLI; fixed by splitting into hub plus references and a check-skill-currency guard. *Fix:* Restructured skill, VERBS[] single source, currency check in predev/pretest.
- **ENH-204** (bug): With the rail filtered to project A, a new terminal with cwd outside A was hidden at birth although it was the active tab. *Fix:* Effect releases focus to All when a new terminal's membership != focusedProject (membership-based, boot-quiet).
- **ENH-207** (feature): Dragging navigator rows onto the terminal inserted their absolute paths at the cursor, quoted when needed, in tree order, with a trailing space and no newline. *Fix:* Shipped in v0.10.0 (PR #81) using HTML5 drag and pty.write.
- **ENH-208** (feature): Typed entity notes, default-vault picker, shift-cmd-N inbox capture, cmd-shift-F vault search palette, graph model. *Fix:* shipped 0.10.x
- **ENH-209** (followup): In legacy, the only way to kill a PTY was the React TerminalInstance unmount cleanup, so any accidental unmount killed the shell. BUG-200 fixed the collapse case, and ENH-209 was filed as the structural fix. *Fix:* Unfixed (a deferred sketch).
- **ENH-210** (feature): Git-worktree tab chips, worktree badges, navigator worktree switcher, open-worktree-in-new-window; a faint 'you are here' pill on the parent project tile in All mode. *Fix:* Shipped (PR #93 and others).
- **ENH-211** (bug): Every file-system event deleted the parent folder's cached listing and refetched it, so the tree showed 'Loading…' for a frame. A write in the cwd blanked the whole tree, and a burst of writes flashed constantly. Expanding a folder tore down the watcher and blanked every folder. Git ticks re-rendered every row. *Fix:* P0 shipped: stale-while-revalidate (never null a loaded listing) plus 100ms coalescing. P1, perf-only, deferred.
- **ENH-212** (feature): Permanent Home tab for re-entering inactive projects and sessions: greeting, hero panels, project list. *Fix:* Shipped v0.10.x.
- **ENH-213** (feature): Opening the ⌘⇧F vault-search palette over a canvas page or browser tab showed bleed-through from the composited surface behind the DOM overlay. The mute covered only the browser WCV, not the canvas iframe. *Fix:* Unfixed. Filed as a P2 polish item after a walk.
- **ENH-214** (feature): ⌘⇧F skipped the templates/ directory because it reused the corpus skip-set, so templates couldn't be found or opened. Fix: search got its own skip-set plus an isTemplate flag, shown as an inline 'Template' badge. *Fix:* Shipped 2026-06-15 (af9434a), walk-3 PASS.
- **ENH-215** (feature): A note with 'type:' should open with its frontmatter panel expanded because the fields are the entity's data. *Fix:* filed only; superseded by ENH-240 (default expanded for all)
- **ENH-216** (feature): Vaults serialize as OKF (relative markdown links plus index.md marker) or Obsidian wikilinks, chosen per vault. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-217** (feature): Home refreshed on 30s pollers but had no in-view refresh control, so closing a session elsewhere or a just-launched session lagged. *Fix:* Backlog (P3); CLI verb existed.
- **ENH-219** (feature): Copy path gave no signal that the file lives in a linked worktree vs the main checkout. *Fix:* unfixed (P3)
- **ENH-220** (feature): Jump the navigator to the enclosing vault root of the active note, plus a CLI twin. *Fix:* filed only (P2)
- **ENH-221** (feature): User said undo was impossible with 800ms autosave. Legacy added a content-addressed append-only store of every save (90s burst coalescing, 200/file cap), a History modal with diff and restore, and duo history list/show/restore. Also fixed Cmd+Z being eaten by a global chord. *Fix:* Shipped v0.11.2: file-history-service plus history modal and CLI.
- **ENH-222** (feature): Create a git worktree from navigator with slug preview; recover gracefully when a rooted worktree is removed. *Fix:* Shipped with `duo worktree new/remove`.
- **ENH-223** (feature): Create and manage scheduled Claude runs from Home: presets or cron, English preview, background launch, run-once-if-missed. *Fix:* Shipped v0.11.x.
- **ENH-224** (feature): Open bar accepts a GitHub URL, managed shallow checkout, edit, Propose changes footer pushes a branch and opens a PR, forking if no push access; duo pr create needs --yes. *Fix:* shipped v0.11.2
- **ENH-225** (feature): Stop/Notification hooks set an amber dot on a background session's tab, cleared on focus or UserPromptSubmit. *Fix:* Shipped v0.11.2 with DUO_TAB env stamp.
- **ENH-226** (feature): Cmd-O opened the Open bar without putting the caret in the URL field. *Fix:* Filed only. The acceptance criterion was focus with the text selected.
- **ENH-227** (bug): PR success was a muted 'Proposed' and View PR used window.open, which silently no-ops in the web view; fixed with PR number and openExternal. *Fix:* shipped: explicit success line, PR number, shell.openExternal path.
- **ENH-228** (feature): Pinned Vault tab with inbox column and rollups column, rendered HTML rollups and discoverability for agents. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-229** (feature): duo rollup renders a base over live frontmatter as GitHub-portable MD or HTML artifact. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-230** (feature): index.md frontmatter listing spec rendered by rollup engine on publish. *Fix:* shipped
- **ENH-231** (feature): Second Home mode: commingled timeline / three-column board of session briefing cards from pre-hydrated digests, zero inference at open. *Fix:* Shipped v0.12.x.
- **ENH-232** (followup): A session whose cwd (worktree) was removed cannot be resumed: claude --resume fails with 'No conversation found' because transcript lives under the encoded cwd dir. *Fix:* Stopgap: card struck through and re-entry disabled; rich fix open.
- **ENH-233** (feature): Let the user drop a reviewed session off the board via a Duo-owned dismissed/reviewedAt annotation (with GC concerns). *Fix:* Open ENH.
- **ENH-234** (feature): Agents could not find or use rollups in an OKF vault; docs contradicted each other; a managed skill was proposed. *Fix:* shipped docs reconciliation (#110)
- **ENH-235** (feature): CLI verb scaffolds a valid .base so agents stop hand-writing rollup YAML. *Fix:* filed only (P2)
- **ENH-236** (bug): After Send to agent from a playground in browser mode, keyboard focus stayed in the page, so typing went to the wrong place. Sends from a doc or canvas were fine. *Fix:* The shared handler skipped reclaimFocus. It was replaced with the proven focusPane('terminal') helper (v0.10.x, #113).
- **ENH-237** (feature): A second cron job kind that runs a plain shell command in a background terminal tab without a Claude session. *Fix:* Shipped #112; follow-ups: no kind badge in job list, no load-time validation.
- **ENH-240** (feature): View-menu setting for whether the frontmatter Properties panel opens expanded; default flipped to expanded. *Fix:* shipped 0.13.1
- **ENH-241** (feature): Wikilinks and markdown links in frontmatter values became clickable in the properties panel. *Fix:* shipped 0.13.1
- **ENH-242** (feature): Picking a non-vault folder as default vault now offers to initialize it, with a data-safety guard. *Fix:* shipped 0.13.2
- **ENH-243** (feature): A GUI for building and viewing Obsidian Bases rollups over the vault. *Fix:* Shipped in legacy 0.13.x (rollups).
- **ENH-244** (feature): Copy a rendered rollup as Markdown. *Fix:* Shipped in legacy 0.13.x (rollups).
- **ENH-245** (bug): Duo hardcoded index.md; the owner's vault used _index.md, which broke publish. Fixed by detecting either and defaulting to underscore. *Fix:* shipped: detect both per vault, one shared helper
- **ENH-246** (feature): Renamed vault's rendered-artifact folder with dual convention (output/ or legacy out/). *Fix:* Shipped 2026-07-02.
- **ENH-247** (feature): Full user manual for the rollup ecosystem. *Fix:* Shipped (CHANGELOG).
- **ENH-248** (feature): Nine owner-walk UX gaps in the rollup GUI: staleness explanation, toolbar, rename/share lifecycle, Vault tab entities section. *Fix:* Shipped across rollup batch.
- **ENH-249** (followup): The owner wanted a human review of docs rewritten by bots for rollup/vault features. *Fix:* Unfixed (filed).
- **ENH-250** (feature): Resolved duplicate navigation between two rollup surfaces; auto-render on save. *Fix:* Shipped 2026-07-02.
- **ENH-251** (feature): Changed the default of the new-rollup button. *Fix:* Shipped 2026-07-03.
- **ENH-253** (feature): Pull modal: fast-forward when clean, auto-merge when diverged (conflicts abort untouched), refuse on dirty tree with explicit discard override; duo pull CLI. *Fix:* shipped v0.13.4
- **ENH-255** (feature): contains/containsAny/containsAll filters, declared group buckets, filter errors shown in output. *Fix:* Shipped #123.
- **ENH-257** (bug): Home, Vault and Rollups tabs had no CLI path; duo view duo://rollups silently created a broken tab. *Fix:* New goto verb with clear error when no default vault.
- **ENH-258** (feature): Filter/group by link-valued properties; pickers offered entities rather than raw link strings. *Fix:* Shipped #126.
- **ENH-259** (feature): Transitive parent-chain filter in rollup builder. *Fix:* Shipped #127.
- **ENH-260** (feature): In TipTap Suggesting mode, deleting your own pending insertion stacked a deletion mark on an insertion mark, giving phantom deletions and a corrupt reject baseline. Fixed with composition rules. *Fix:* Shipped 2026-07-13 (PR #129): transaction rewrite so own-insertion deletes net to zero.
- **ENH-261** (feature): Group rows by nearest ancestor of a given type. *Fix:* Shipped #128.
- **ENH-262** (feature): Pseudo-property filtering on any link to an entity. *Fix:* Shipped #128.
- **ENH-263** (feature): New OKF vaults opened in Obsidian already in markdown-link mode (useMarkdownLinks) so interop works. *Fix:* Shipped #130.
- **ENH-264** (followup): Owner-review HTML playground comparing four schema options for his work KB. *Fix:* Awaiting owner walk; research artifact only.
- **ENH-266** (feature): Frontmatter entity refs in OKF mode written as quoted markdown relative links so vanilla Obsidian resolves them instead of creating phantom nodes; auto-migration on vault open. *Fix:* Shipped (PR family #130-#132).
- **ENH-266a** (feature): [[ pick in frontmatter writes quoted markdown link; opt-in migration. *Fix:* Shipped #131.
- **ENH-266c** (feature): Same shipped change as ENH-263: seeds app.json for new OKF vaults. *Fix:* Shipped #130.
- **ENH-266d** (bug): Template notes appeared as rows in rollups/Bases; lint check and scaffold exclusion added. *Fix:* Shipped #132 (lint D15 plus scaffold filter).
- **ENH-266e** (feature): Notes get aliases frontmatter from title so quick-switcher finds them. *Fix:* Shipped #132.
- **ENH-267** (followup): Only mention is a note in the ENH-182 PRD that BUG-267 collided with ENH-267, so the bug was renumbered BUG-269 (D11 adjudicating activations, not focus changes, to stop focus flicker). *Fix:* Renumbered; fix never merged.
- **FOLLOWUP-001** (followup): Process: every new CLI verb must update the agent cheat-sheet or agents will not know it. *Fix:* Closed: checklist item 7 updated.
- **FOLLOWUP-002** (bug): Agent's guard command ([ -n $DUO_SESSION ]) was permission-denied under a tight Bash(duo *) allowlist, and the agent proceeded anyway outside Duo. *Fix:* Open: proposed prompt wording to treat an unrunnable guard as not-in-Duo and stop.
- **FOLLOWUP-003** (followup): A synthetic benchmark showed the duo subagent path costing about twice the inline path. The owner wanted a 10-task cumulative-context re-measure. *Fix:* Unfixed (open, low priority).
- **FOLLOWUP-004** (followup): A visual smoke test was deferred because the computer-use access request timed out in the spawning session. *Fix:* Unfixed; to be covered by a walk by eye.
- **FOLLOWUP-005** (followup): Helper scripts for the signed and notarized DMG deferred until the macOS keychain prompt for the Developer ID cert could be handled. *Fix:* Shipped later as dist-signed.sh and validate-signed-dmg.sh.
- **FOLLOWUP-006** (followup): Autosave at about 800ms cleared the dirty state before the owner could try the dirty-replace dialog in a smoke walk. Asked for an env or CLI knob. *Fix:* Unfixed in the section read (open, low-priority test tooling).
- **FOLLOWUP-007** (followup): Worksheet pages call window.duoSendResult(text, opts), which did not exist, so only the clipboard fallback worked. *Fix:* Binding plumbing to be wired via CDP (pre-req for ENH-093 host:send-to-claude); not shipped.
- **FOLLOWUP-008** (followup): Tailwind opacity modifiers silently failed on colours defined as var() hex values. *Fix:* Added RGB triplet tokens with <alpha-value>.
- **FOLLOWUP-009** (followup): A comment re-anchoring bug (BUG-082/088) had no durable regression test. The plan was to add the React test infrastructure. *Fix:* Partially addressed v0.6.8: pivoted to jsdom tests of the pure pieces (idInjector, markdownComments), without testing-library.
- **FOLLOWUP-010** (followup): Deferred network install of distro packs from a URL. *Fix:* Unfiled/deferred; adds network/security model.
- **FOLLOWUP-011** (followup): Deferred cross-machine control-socket auth; later also a pack builder walk on another Mac. *Fix:* Deferred until a real use case.
- **FOLLOWUP-012** (followup): Toast like 'your agent just navigated to /path/foo.md' when an agent opens something in the UI. *Fix:* Deferred, not distro-specific.
- **FOLLOWUP-013** (followup): Right-click Move to Split View crashed the Electron renderer. It was never reproduced cleanly. *Fix:* Unfixed; instrumentation only.
- **FOLLOWUP-014** (bug): Pasted images were inserted as absolute custom-protocol or blob URLs, so docs broke when moved. Walk-2 also found a re-baseline race that silently dropped canvas saves, and an IPC race that put the image in the wrong open tab. *Fix:* v0.6.11: relative filenames in source, NodeView resolves via files.read; baselinedRef gate; isActive gate on IPC subscriptions.
- **FOLLOWUP-015** (feature): Legacy had a modal showing the raw source (markdown with frontmatter, or HTML) on Cmd-Opt-V. v2 reshaped it into a read-only panel that fills the pane, with View menu and tab right-click entries. *Fix:* Shipped v0.6.12.
- **FOLLOWUP-019** (followup): HTML canvas lacked the markdown editor's external-write handling: watcher, pre-save reconciliation, echo guards; edits could clobber agent writes. *Fix:* Shipped Sprint 16 by mirroring watcher, pre-save check and 2s write-echo map into PageTab.
- **FOLLOWUP-020** (feature): No CLI counterpart to cmd-W for the working-pane file tab or terminal tab; agents left tabs piling up. *Fix:* Shipped duo close-tab and close-terminal-tab with pinned-tab gating.
- **FOLLOWUP-021** (feature): Opt-in cleanup of vestigial rc fences and stale ~/.local/bin/duo symlinks that confused diagnosis. *Fix:* Open, low priority, gated on user pain.
- **FOLLOWUP-022** (feature): Legacy's CriticMarkup HighlightMark ({==X==}, part of the BUG-138 comments/track-changes chapter) existed in the editor but the CLI had no highlight verb alongside insert, delete, substitute, comment. *Fix:* Shipped duo doc highlight in v0.7.2 (tasks-archive.md:2473).
- **FOLLOWUP-023** (bug): After duo doc comment --reply-to, the chokidar reload re-applied CriticMarkup marks on an already-marked buffer, so comment highlights showed as insertion cards until the tab was reopened. *Fix:* Unfixed (low); workaround was close-reopen, documented in the skill.
- **FOLLOWUP-024** (bug): Pasted images went in at the caret inline, giving '![](x.png)text' on one line, which GitHub renders badly. *Fix:* Shipped v0.7.5: DuoImage node made block-level.
- **FOLLOWUP-025** (followup): The renderer modal for clone: right-click Clone GitHub repo here, default dir from Navigator, progress and success panels. *Fix:* shipped
- **FOLLOWUP-026** (followup): The Electron IPC `files.openExternal` wrapped `shell.openPath` (local files) but its name suggested the URL opener. One caller passed URLs to it and failed silently. It was filed as a clarity refactor during BUG-132. *Fix:* Shipped 2026-05-17 in v0.7.0 prep. The IPC channel, the FilesService method, preload and 3 call sites were renamed to openPath, and `openExternalUrl` was kept for URLs.
- **FOLLOWUP-027** (followup): duo open <url> created an about:blank ghost tab while the system browser popped with the real URL. *Fix:* open/navigate return routedTo:'system-browser' without creating the tab.
- **FOLLOWUP-028** (followup): Auto-injecting '\r/rename <title>' could force-submit the user's partial input mid-turn. *Fix:* T3 dropped in the pare-back; design no longer needed.
- **FOLLOWUP-029** (followup): Idea note: a distro pack could ship as a pre-declared project (CLAUDE.md, skills, starting tabs) and 'focus' would drop an enterprise user into it. *Fix:* unfixed; parked until distro-pack work resumes.
- **FOLLOWUP-030** (followup): Entering a project focus hid non-member browser tabs from the strip but the pane still rendered the active non-member tab's content. *Fix:* Shipped: redirect to a true member tab or fall back to a file tab.
- **FOLLOWUP-031** (bug): Each TerminalPane registered its own IPC listener for presence changes, so with more than 10 tabs Electron's EventEmitter leak warning fired. *Fix:* Planned fix: one app-level subscription shared through context. Filed as High, about 30 minutes of work.
- **FOLLOWUP-032** (bug): Two parallel CLI close calls each ran handleCloseProject and stacked two confirm dialogs. *Fix:* Proposed in-flight ref guard.
- **FOLLOWUP-033** (bug): Before the first renderer push (1-2 s) the CLI saw an empty projects state, indistinguishable from none. *Fix:* Proposed ready flag plus warning.
- **FOLLOWUP-034** (followup): Hash(path)%6 rail colour collides often; PRD left the shade rule for >6 projects undecided (options A-D, default defer). *Fix:* Unfixed; defer.
- **FOLLOWUP-035** (followup): Audit flagged a possibly unused callback in legacy App.tsx. *Fix:* Five-minute grep and delete.
- **FOLLOWUP-036** (followup): The chip had visible text and an aria-label that repeated it. *Fix:* Filed: shorten the label.
- **FOLLOWUP-037** (followup): The renderer cached per-candidate-dir marker probes (CLAUDE.md) for the whole session. If a pinned project's CLAUDE.md was deleted outside Duo, the rail kept a ghost tile until relaunch. Filed as a Tier 3 owner decision with four options. *Fix:* Unfixed. The default was Option D: leave as-is and document it. Low priority.
- **FOLLOWUP-038** (bug): A React hook's event filter used `'key' in event`, which would also filter a CustomEvent that carried a `key` field. Benign because Duo's own events had none. The fix was a code comment. *Fix:* Unfixed. It was only to get a comment in a Tier 1 polish commit.
- **FOLLOWUP-039** (bug): The pill-menu flag lived in localStorage. A toggle in one window fired a `storage` event in the other windows but the in-window CustomEvent only in the origin window, so the flag could be out of sync across windows. It became reachable once multi-window shipped in v0.10.0. *Fix:* Unfixed. Option A (document, defer BroadcastChannel) was the default.
- **FOLLOWUP-040** (followup): After the workspace pill became a passive label (ENH-184), a smoke-walk manifest item was filed to check that File > New Workspace still worked with the pill menu disabled. It was verification only, with no bug found. *Fix:* Shipped as a manifest entry (verification-only).
- **FOLLOWUP-041** (bug): When a project's cwd was deleted, the terminal recovered (BUG-165), but the navigator's `files:list` IPC still hit ENOENT on scandir. The tree came up empty or errored and was never rebound to a surviving ancestor. *Fix:* Unfixed when filed. The proposed fix reused `resolveExistingCwd` to walk up to the nearest surviving ancestor, rebind the tree there and show a chip note.
- **FOLLOWUP-042** (feature): Terminal-tab reorder (menu and drag) had no CLI verb, a known parity asymmetry. *Fix:* Open, low priority.
- **FOLLOWUP-043** (bug): Dropping a navigator row on the collapsed 36px terminal rail expanded the pane but spawned a new terminal and did not insert the path. *Fix:* Unfixed (Low, not blocking). The cause was the drop handler on the column wrapper racing a stale activeTabId.
- **FOLLOWUP-044** (bug): Collapsing the canvas unmounted WorkingPane, possibly losing editor and browser state. *Fix:* Unverified. The suggested fix was display:none, as in BUG-200.
- **FOLLOWUP-045** (feature): duo split was clamped to 20-80%, so the agent could not collapse a pane. *Fix:* Filed: add a collapse verb.
- **FOLLOWUP-046** (bug): Legacy CLI 'duo base render' on an empty or comments-only .base file warned and rendered; changed to a clean stderr one-liner and exit 1 (non-blocking, no crash or data loss). *Fix:* Shipped in legacy v0.10.1 as a CLI exit-path change.
- **FOLLOWUP-047** (followup): Cleanup of unreachable window listeners (duo-page-find-prev, duo-browser-find-prev) left after the global ⌘⇧F find-previous was retired. It also documented a deliberate asymmetry: the browser find bar is ⇧Enter-only. *Fix:* Unfixed, P3 dead code with no user impact.
- **FOLLOWUP-048** (feature): Legacy body editor's [[ suggester matcher rejected spaces, so the New: row/type picker only worked for single-word entities; multi-word went through the CLI. *Fix:* Unfixed; sketch was to widen the matcher with Obsidian-style dismissal rules.
- **FOLLOWUP-049** (followup): Deferred from ENH-211. The navigator tore down and rebuilt the chokidar watcher on every expand or collapse, re-rendered every row on each git tick, and leaked `files:changed` listeners (MaxListenersExceededWarning). Live checks showed no visible flicker, only wasted CPU. *Fix:* Unfixed, filed as P2 perf. The plan was `updateWatchPaths` deltas (D3), a return-prev guard on the git Maps (D5) and React.memo rows (D4).
- **FOLLOWUP-050** (bug): Legacy frontmatter raw-YAML textarea only rewrote [[Name]] to a rel-path on commit if the note already existed; no live autocomplete, type-picker or create flow. *Fix:* Added a textarea-anchored [[ popover reusing the body suggester pieces (useFrontmatterWikilink, textareaCaret).
- **FOLLOWUP-051** (bug): Legacy OKF mode stored frontmatter links as quoted rel-paths, which Duo's graph regexes and Obsidian ignore (orphans); flipped to [[basename]] in both modes. *Fix:* Shipped: frontmatter [[ ]] persists as [[ ]]; rewrite code deleted.
- **FOLLOWUP-052** (feature): Legacy Properties panel showed frontmatter [[name]] values as plain text; owner wanted click or cmd-click to open the target note. *Fix:* Unfixed (filed, P2).
- **FOLLOWUP-053** (followup): Two legacy ENH-216 walk items (flip vault dialect changes the [[ ]] serializer; open the OKF vault in Obsidian and navigate rel links) were skipped through three walks; owner-eyes verification only. *Fix:* Never verified; non-blocking.
- **FOLLOWUP-054** (followup): Eleven pre-existing findings were documented but not fixed: Home open-session attribution by live cwd could fork a live session; navigator refetch dropped on collapse; a min-cols floor dropped narrow resizes; loose UUID regex; worktree probe cost; an ENH number collision. *Fix:* Unfixed (documentation only, v0.11.0).
